# Dhammapada

`src:dhammapada` · `skeleton` · confidence high

**Language:** Pali
**Family:** ascetic
**Lineages:** [Early Buddhism (the Dhamma-Vinaya of the Nikāyas)](../lineages/early-buddhism.md), [Theravāda](../lineages/theravada.md)
**Genre:** verse anthology
**Part of:** [Khuddaka Nikāya](khuddaka-nikaya.md)
**Dates:** Scholarly account: c. 3rd c. BCE (compilation); parallels in the Gāndhārī Dharmapada, Patna Dharmapada and Udānavarga; (confidence moderate)
**Structure:** 26 vaggas, 423 verses
**Availability:** digitized-original

423 verses in 26 chapters on the Dhamma as a way of life: mind as forerunner (1–2), hatred ceased by non-hatred (5), heedfulness, the mind, the fool and the wise, the arahant, the self, the world, the Buddha (183), happiness, craving, the path (277–279), the bhikkhu and the true brahmin.
**Editions / translations:** 
  - kind: original; name: Mahāsaṅgīti Tipiṭaka Buddhavasse 2500 (World Tipiṭaka edition, based on the Sixth Council text), as published by SuttaCentral in bilara-data (root/pli/ms); local copy in sources_raw/bilara-data; licence: CC0 / public domain as published by SuttaCentral; url: https://suttacentral.net/dhp
**Commentaries on this text:** [Dhammapada-aṭṭhakathā](dhammapada-atthakatha.md)

## Teachings (454: text-verified 450, skeleton 4)

### 1 <a id="tea-dhammapada-1"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Things (dhammā) have mind (mano) as their forerunner, mind as their chief, and are made of mind (manomayā). If one speaks or acts with a corrupted mind, suffering (dukkha) follows one, as the wheel follows the foot of the ox that draws the cart.

> Manopubbaṅgamā dhammā, manoseṭṭhā manomayā; Manasā ce paduṭṭhena, bhāsati vā karoti vā; Tato naṁ dukkhamanveti, cakkaṁva vahato padaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [mano](../terms/mano.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md) · concepts: [Mind as forerunner of things (manopubbaṅgamā dhammā)](../concepts/mano-pubbangama.md)

### 1-2 <a id="tea-dhammapada-1-2"></a>
`skeleton` · confidence high

Mind is the forerunner of all states; mind is chief, they are mind-made. If one speaks or acts with a corrupted mind, suffering follows as the wheel follows the ox's foot; if with a pure mind, happiness follows like a shadow that never leaves.

> Manopubbaṅgamā dhammā, manoseṭṭhā manomayā; Manasā ce paduṭṭhena, bhāsati vā karoti vā; Tato naṁ dukkhamanveti, cakkaṁva vahato padaṁ. Manopubbaṅgamā dhammā, manoseṭṭhā manomayā; Manasā ce pasannena, bhāsati vā karoti vā; Tato naṁ sukhamanveti, chāyāva anapāyinī.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: action · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [mano](../terms/mano.md), [cetana](../terms/cetana.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md)

_Superseded by [1](dhammapada.md#tea-dhammapada-1)_

### 2 <a id="tea-dhammapada-2"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Things (dhammā) have mind (mano) as their forerunner, mind as their chief, and are made of mind. If one speaks or acts with a clear, serene mind (pasanna), happiness (sukha) follows one, like a shadow that never departs.

> Maṭṭhakuṇḍalīvatthu Manopubbaṅgamā dhammā, manoseṭṭhā manomayā; Manasā ce pasannena, bhāsati vā karoti vā; Tato naṁ sukhamanveti, chāyāva anapāyinī.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [mano](../terms/mano.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [pasāda](../terms/pasada.md) · concepts: [Mind as forerunner of things (manopubbaṅgamā dhammā)](../concepts/mano-pubbangama.md)

### 3 <a id="tea-dhammapada-3"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'He abused me, he struck me, he defeated me, he robbed me': for those who harbour (upanayhanti) such thoughts, enmity (vera) is not stilled.

> Tissattheravatthu Akkocchi maṁ avadhi maṁ, ajini maṁ ahāsi me; Ye ca taṁ upanayhanti, veraṁ tesaṁ na sammati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

terms: [vera](../terms/vera.md), [upanāha](../terms/upanaha.md) · obstacles: [Harbouring resentment (upanayhati)](../obstacles/upanaha.md)

### 4 <a id="tea-dhammapada-4"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'He abused me, he struck me, he defeated me, he robbed me': for those who do not harbour such thoughts, enmity (vera) is stilled.

> Akkocchi maṁ avadhi maṁ, ajini maṁ ahāsi me; Ye ca taṁ nupanayhanti, veraṁ tesūpasammati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

terms: [vera](../terms/vera.md), [upanāha](../terms/upanaha.md) · obstacles: [Harbouring resentment (upanayhati)](../obstacles/upanaha.md)

### 5 <a id="tea-dhammapada-5"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Enmities (verāni) are never stilled in this world by enmity (vera); they are stilled by non-enmity (avera). This is an ancient, perennial dhamma (sanantana dhamma).

> Kāḷayakkhinīvatthu Na hi verena verāni, sammantīdha kudācanaṁ; Averena ca sammanti, esa dhammo sanantano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general, action · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [vera](../terms/vera.md), [avera](../terms/avera.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md) · concepts: [Non-hatred](../concepts/non-hatred.md)

### 6 <a id="tea-dhammapada-6"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Others do not understand that we here should restrain ourselves (yamāmase); but for those there who do understand it, quarrels (medhagā) are settled from that.

> Kosambakavatthu Pare ca na vijānanti, mayamettha yamāmase; Ye ca tattha vijānanti, tato sammanti medhagā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

### 7 <a id="tea-dhammapada-7"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who lives contemplating the beautiful (subhānupassī), with the sense faculties (indriya) unrestrained, immoderate in food, lazy (kusīta) and of poor energy (hīnavīriya): Māra overpowers that person as the wind a weak tree.

> Mahākāḷattheravatthu Subhānupassiṁ viharantaṁ, indriyesu asaṁvutaṁ; Bhojanamhi cāmattaññuṁ, kusītaṁ hīnavīriyaṁ; Taṁ ve pasahati māro, vāto rukkhaṁva dubbalaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: beginner · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [subha](../terms/subha.md), [indriya](../terms/indriya.md), [indriyasaṃvara](../terms/indriya-samvara.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [viriya](../terms/viriya.md) · practices: [Sense restraint (indriyasaṃvara)](../practices/indriya-samvara.md), [Moderation in food (mattaññutā)](../practices/mattannuta.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 8 <a id="tea-dhammapada-8"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who lives contemplating the unlovely (asubhānupassī), with the sense faculties well restrained, moderate in food, faithful (saddha) and with energy roused (āraddhavīriya): Māra does not overpower that person, as the wind does not a rocky mountain.

> Asubhānupassiṁ viharantaṁ, Indriyesu susaṁvutaṁ; Bhojanamhi ca mattaññuṁ, Saddhaṁ āraddhavīriyaṁ; Taṁ ve nappasahati māro, Vāto selaṁva pabbataṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: beginner · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [asubha](../terms/asubha.md), [indriya](../terms/indriya.md), [indriyasaṃvara](../terms/indriya-samvara.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [saddhā](../terms/saddha.md), [viriya](../terms/viriya.md) · practices: [Sense restraint (indriyasaṃvara)](../practices/indriya-samvara.md), [Contemplation of the foul (asubha-bhāvanā): summary only](../practices/asubha-bhavana.md), [Moderation in food (mattaññutā)](../practices/mattannuta.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 9 <a id="tea-dhammapada-9"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who, not having removed the stains (anikkasāva), would put on the ochre robe (kāsāva), devoid of self-control (dama) and truthfulness (sacca): that one is not worthy of the ochre robe.

> Devadattavatthu Anikkasāvo kāsāvaṁ, yo vatthaṁ paridahissati; Apeto damasaccena, na so kāsāvamarahati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [kasāva](../terms/kasava.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md), [sacca](../terms/sacca.md)

### 10 <a id="tea-dhammapada-10"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who has vomited out the stains (vantakasāva), well composed in the ethical precepts (sīla), endowed with self-control (dama) and truthfulness (sacca): that one is indeed worthy of the ochre robe.

> Yo ca vantakasāvassa, sīlesu susamāhito; Upeto damasaccena, sa ve kāsāvamarahati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [kasāva](../terms/kasava.md), [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md), [sacca](../terms/sacca.md) · concepts: [Virtue (sīla)](../concepts/sila.md)

### 11 <a id="tea-dhammapada-11"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Those who take the unessential (asāra) to be essential (sāra) and see the essential as unessential do not reach the essential; wrong intention (micchāsaṅkappa) is their pasture (gocara).

> Sāriputtattheravatthu Asāre sāramatino, sāre cāsāradassino; Te sāraṁ nādhigacchanti, micchāsaṅkappagocarā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [saṅkappa](../terms/sankappa.md)

### 12 <a id="tea-dhammapada-12"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Those who know the essential as essential and the unessential as unessential reach the essential; right intention (sammāsaṅkappa) is their pasture (gocara).

> Sārañca sārato ñatvā, asārañca asārato; Te sāraṁ adhigacchanti, sammāsaṅkappagocarā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [saṅkappa](../terms/sankappa.md)

### 13 <a id="tea-dhammapada-13"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As rain breaks through a badly roofed house, so passion (rāga) breaks through an undeveloped mind (abhāvita citta).

> Nandattheravatthu Yathā agāraṁ ducchannaṁ, vuṭṭhī samativijjhati; Evaṁ abhāvitaṁ cittaṁ, rāgo samativijjhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [rāga](../terms/raga.md) · obstacles: [Passion (rāga)](../obstacles/raga-pali.md)

### 14 <a id="tea-dhammapada-14"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As rain does not break through a well-roofed house, so passion (rāga) does not break through a well-developed mind (subhāvita citta).

> Yathā agāraṁ suchannaṁ, vuṭṭhī na samativijjhati; Evaṁ subhāvitaṁ cittaṁ, rāgo na samativijjhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [rāga](../terms/raga.md) · obstacles: [Passion (rāga)](../obstacles/raga-pali.md)

### 15 <a id="tea-dhammapada-15"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Here one grieves (socati), having departed one grieves: the evildoer grieves in both places. One grieves and is distressed, seeing the defilement of one's own deeds (kammakiliṭṭha).

> Cundasūkarikavatthu Idha socati pecca socati, Pāpakārī ubhayattha socati; So socati so vihaññati, Disvā kammakiliṭṭhamattano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, world-fate_

terms: [soka](../terms/soka.md), [kamma](../terms/kamma.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md) · obstacles: [Grief (soka)](../obstacles/soka-pali.md)

### 16 <a id="tea-dhammapada-16"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Here one rejoices (modati), having departed one rejoices: the one who has made merit (katapuñña) rejoices in both places. One rejoices and is delighted, seeing the purity of one's own deeds (kammavisuddhi).

> Dhammikaupāsakavatthu Idha modati pecca modati, Katapuñño ubhayattha modati; So modati so pamodati, Disvā kammavisuddhimattano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, world-fate_

terms: [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [kamma](../terms/kamma.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md)

### 17 <a id="tea-dhammapada-17"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Here one is tormented (tappati), having departed one is tormented: the evildoer is tormented in both places. 'Evil has been done by me,' one is tormented; gone to a bad destination (duggati), one is tormented still more.

> Devadattavatthu Idha tappati pecca tappati, Pāpakārī ubhayattha tappati; “Pāpaṁ me katan”ti tappati, Bhiyyo tappati duggatiṁ gato.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, world-fate_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [duggati](../terms/duggati.md)

### 18 <a id="tea-dhammapada-18"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Here one delights (nandati), having departed one delights: the one who has made merit is glad in both places. 'Merit has been done by me,' one is glad; gone to a good destination (suggati), one is glad still more.

> Sumanadevīvatthu Idha nandati pecca nandati, Katapuñño ubhayattha nandati; “Puññaṁ me katan”ti nandati, Bhiyyo nandati suggatiṁ gato.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, world-fate_

terms: [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [suggati](../terms/suggati.md)

### 19 <a id="tea-dhammapada-19"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even if one recites much of the texts (saṃhitā), the heedless (pamatta) person who does not act accordingly is like a cowherd counting the cattle of others: he has no share in the ascetic life (sāmañña).

> Dvesahāyakabhikkhuvatthu Bahumpi ce saṁhita bhāsamāno, Na takkaro hoti naro pamatto; Gopova gāvo gaṇayaṁ paresaṁ, Na bhāgavā sāmaññassa hoti.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [sāmañña](../terms/samanna.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 20 <a id="tea-dhammapada-20"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even if one recites little of the texts, if one lives in line with the Dhamma (dhammassa anudhammacārī), having abandoned passion (rāga), hatred (dosa) and delusion (moha), clearly comprehending (sammappajāna), with a well-freed mind (suvimutta citta), not clinging (anupādiyāna) here or hereafter, one has a share in the ascetic life (sāmañña).

> Appampi ce saṁhita bhāsamāno, Dhammassa hoti anudhammacārī; Rāgañca dosañca pahāya mohaṁ, Sammappajāno suvimuttacitto; Anupādiyāno idha vā huraṁ vā, Sa bhāgavā sāmaññassa hoti. Yamakavaggo paṭhamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: realized · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [rāga](../terms/raga.md), [doṣa](../terms/dosa.md), [moha](../terms/moha.md), [sāmañña](../terms/samanna.md), [citta](../terms/citta.md) · obstacles: [Passion (rāga)](../obstacles/raga-pali.md), [Hate (dosa)](../obstacles/dosa.md), [Delusion (moha)](../obstacles/moha.md)

### 21 <a id="tea-dhammapada-21"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Heedfulness (appamāda) is the path/state of the deathless (amatapada); heedlessness (pamāda) is the path/state of death (maccu). The heedful do not die; the heedless are as if already dead.

> Appamādo amatapadaṁ, pamādo maccuno padaṁ; Appamattā na mīyanti, ye pamattā yathā matā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [amata](../terms/amata.md), [maccu](../terms/maccu.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 22 <a id="tea-dhammapada-22"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Having understood this distinction with regard to heedfulness, the wise (paṇḍita) rejoice in heedfulness, delighting in the domain (gocara) of the noble ones (ariya).

> Evaṁ visesato ñatvā, appamādamhi paṇḍitā; Appamāde pamodanti, ariyānaṁ gocare ratā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [ariya](../terms/ariya.md), [gocara](../terms/gocara.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md)

### 23 <a id="tea-dhammapada-23"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Those who are meditators (jhāyī), constant (sātatika), ever firm in their exertion, the steadfast (dhīra) touch nibbāna, the unsurpassed security from bondage (yogakkhema).

> Te jhāyino sātatikā, niccaṁ daḷhaparakkamā; Phusanti dhīrā nibbānaṁ, yogakkhemaṁ anuttaraṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [jhāna](../terms/jhana.md), [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md), [yogakkhema](../terms/yogakkhema.md) · concepts: [Nibbāna](../concepts/nibbana.md) · practices: [Meditation (jhāna)](../practices/jhana.md)

### 24 <a id="tea-dhammapada-24"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For one who is energetic (uṭṭhānavant), mindful (satīmant), of clean action, who acts with consideration, who is restrained (saññata), who lives by the Dhamma (dhammajīvī) and is heedful, good repute (yasa) increases.

> Kumbaghosakaseṭṭhivatthu Uṭṭhānavato satīmato, Sucikammassa nisammakārino; Saññatassa dhammajīvino, Appamattassa yasobhivaḍḍhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [sati](../terms/sati.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [yasa](../terms/yasa.md), [uṭṭhāna](../terms/utthana-pali.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md)

### 25 <a id="tea-dhammapada-25"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

By effort (uṭṭhāna), heedfulness, restraint (saṃyama) and self-control (dama), the wise one should make an island (dīpa) that no flood (ogha) can overwhelm.

> Cūḷapanthakavatthu Uṭṭhānenappamādena, saṁyamena damena ca; Dīpaṁ kayirātha medhāvī, yaṁ ogho nābhikīrati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [dīpa](../terms/dipa.md), [ogha](../terms/ogha.md), [saṃyama](../terms/samyama-pali.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md), [uṭṭhāna](../terms/utthana-pali.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md)

### 26 <a id="tea-dhammapada-26"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Fools, people of poor wisdom, devote themselves to heedlessness (pamāda); the wise one guards heedfulness (appamāda) like the best wealth (dhana).

> Bālanakkhattasaṅghuṭṭhavatthu Pamādamanuyuñjanti, bālā dummedhino janā; Appamādañca medhāvī, dhanaṁ seṭṭhaṁva rakkhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [medhāvī](../terms/medhavi.md), [dhana](../terms/dhana.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 27 <a id="tea-dhammapada-27"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Do not devote yourselves to heedlessness (pamāda) nor to intimacy with delight in sense pleasures (kāmaratisanthava); for the heedful one who meditates (jhāyanto) attains abundant happiness (vipulaṁ sukhaṁ).

> Mā pamādamanuyuñjetha, mā kāmaratisanthavaṁ; Appamatto hi jhāyanto, pappoti vipulaṁ sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [kāma](../terms/kama.md), [jhāna](../terms/jhana.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md), [Meditation (jhāna)](../practices/jhana.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 28 <a id="tea-dhammapada-28"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

When the wise one drives away heedlessness by heedfulness, having climbed the palace of wisdom (paññāpāsāda), free of sorrow, he looks on the sorrowing people, as one standing on a mountain looks on those on the ground: the steadfast one looks on fools.

> Mahākassapattheravatthu Pamādaṁ appamādena, yadā nudati paṇḍito; Paññāpāsādamāruyha, asoko sokiniṁ pajaṁ; Pabbataṭṭhova bhūmaṭṭhe, dhīro bāle avekkhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: knowledge · stage: advanced · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md), [Grief (soka)](../obstacles/soka-pali.md)

### 29 <a id="tea-dhammapada-29"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Heedful among the heedless, wide awake among the sleeping, the one of great wisdom (sumedha) goes forward leaving them behind, like a swift horse leaving a weak one.

> Pamattāpamattadvesahāyakabhikkhuvatthu Appamatto pamattesu, suttesu bahujāgaro; Abalassaṁva sīghasso, hitvā yāti sumedhaso.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [medhāvī](../terms/medhavi.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 30 <a id="tea-dhammapada-30"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

By heedfulness Maghavā reached the supremacy among the gods; heedfulness is praised, and heedlessness is always censured.

> Maghavatthu Appamādena maghavā, devānaṁ seṭṭhataṁ gato; Appamādaṁ pasaṁsanti, pamādo garahito sadā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 31 <a id="tea-dhammapada-31"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

A bhikkhu who delights in heedfulness or sees danger in heedlessness goes forward like a fire, burning the fetters (saṃyojana), small and large.

> Aññatarabhikkhuvatthu Appamādarato bhikkhu, pamāde bhayadassi vā; Saṁyojanaṁ aṇuṁ thūlaṁ, ḍahaṁ aggīva gacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: intermediate · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [saṁyojana](../terms/samyojana.md), [bhaya](../terms/bhaya.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 32 <a id="tea-dhammapada-32"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

A bhikkhu who delights in heedfulness or sees danger in heedlessness cannot decline (abhabbo parihānāya); he is in the very presence of nibbāna.

> Nigamavāsitissattheravatthu Appamādarato bhikkhu, pamāde bhayadassi vā; Abhabbo parihānāya, nibbānasseva santike. Appamādavaggo dutiyo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: intermediate · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md) · concepts: [Nibbāna](../concepts/nibbana.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 33 <a id="tea-dhammapada-33"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The mind (citta) is quivering (phandana), unsteady (capala), hard to guard (dūrakkha), hard to restrain (dunnivāraya); the discerning one makes it straight, as a fletcher makes an arrow straight.

> Phandanaṁ capalaṁ cittaṁ, dūrakkhaṁ dunnivārayaṁ; Ujuṁ karoti medhāvī, usukārova tejanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [medhāvī](../terms/medhavi.md) · obstacles: [The unsteady mind (citta)](../obstacles/restless-mind-pali.md)

### 34 <a id="tea-dhammapada-34"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Like a fish (vārija, born of water) pulled from its watery home and thrown on dry land, this mind flutters about, (struggling) to escape the domain of Māra (māradheyya).

> Vārijova thale khitto, okamokataubbhato; Pariphandatidaṁ cittaṁ, māradheyyaṁ pahātave.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [māradheyya](../terms/maradheyya.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 35 <a id="tea-dhammapada-35"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The mind is hard to hold back (dunniggaha), swift (lahuka), alighting where it wishes (yatthakāmanipātin): taming the mind (cittassa damatho) is good; a tamed mind brings happiness.

> Aññatarabhikkhuvatthu Dunniggahassa lahuno, yatthakāmanipātino; Cittassa damatho sādhu, cittaṁ dantaṁ sukhāvahaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [damatha](../terms/damatha.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md) · practices: [Taming the mind (cittassa damatho)](../practices/citta-damatha.md) · obstacles: [The unsteady mind (citta)](../obstacles/restless-mind-pali.md)

### 36 <a id="tea-dhammapada-36"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The mind is very hard to see (sududdasa), very subtle (sunipuṇa), alighting where it wishes; the wise one should guard the mind (cittaṁ rakkhetha); a guarded mind brings happiness.

> Aññataraukkaṇṭhitabhikkhuvatthu Sududdasaṁ sunipuṇaṁ, yatthakāmanipātinaṁ; Cittaṁ rakkhetha medhāvī, cittaṁ guttaṁ sukhāvahaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [medhāvī](../terms/medhavi.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md) · practices: [Guarding the mind (cittaṁ rakkhetha)](../practices/citta-rakkha.md) · obstacles: [The unsteady mind (citta)](../obstacles/restless-mind-pali.md)

### 37 <a id="tea-dhammapada-37"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Far-going (dūraṅgama), going alone (ekacara), bodiless (asarīra), lying in the cave (guhāsaya): those who will restrain the mind are freed from Māra's bonds (mārabandhana).

> Saṅgharakkhitabhāgineyyattheravatthu Dūraṅgamaṁ ekacaraṁ, asarīraṁ guhāsayaṁ; Ye cittaṁ saṁyamissanti, mokkhanti mārabandhanā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [guhā](../terms/guha-pali.md), [bandhana](../terms/bandhana.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 38 <a id="tea-dhammapada-38"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For one whose mind is unsteady (anavaṭṭhitacitta), who does not know the true Dhamma (saddhamma) and whose confidence (pasāda) wavers, wisdom (paññā) does not become full.

> Cittahatthattheravatthu Anavaṭṭhitacittassa, saddhammaṁ avijānato; Pariplavapasādassa, paññā na paripūrati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [saddhamma](../terms/saddhamma.md), [pasāda](../terms/pasada.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md)

### 39 <a id="tea-dhammapada-39"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

For one whose mind is not soaked (anavassutacitta), whose mind is not struck (ananvāhatacetasa), who has abandoned merit and evil (puññapāpapahīna), who is awake (jāgara), there is no fear.

> Anavassutacittassa, ananvāhatacetaso; Puññapāpapahīnassa, natthi jāgarato bhayaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: realized · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [jāgara](../terms/jagara.md), [bhaya](../terms/bhaya.md)

### 40 <a id="tea-dhammapada-40"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Knowing this body to be like a jar (kumbha), making this mind stand like a fortified city (nagara), one should fight Māra with the weapon of wisdom (paññāvudha); one should guard what has been won (jitaṁ) and be without a dwelling-place (anivesana).

> Pañcasatabhikkhuvatthu Kumbhūpamaṁ kāyamimaṁ viditvā, Nagarūpamaṁ cittamidaṁ ṭhapetvā; Yodhetha māraṁ paññāvudhena, Jitañca rakkhe anivesano siyā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: intermediate · types: practice, body-layers_

terms: [kāya](../terms/kaya.md), [citta](../terms/citta.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [anivesana](../terms/anivesana.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 41 <a id="tea-dhammapada-41"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Before long this body will lie on the earth, discarded, bereft of consciousness (apetaviññāṇa), like a useless log (kaliṅgara).

> Pūtigattatissattheravatthu Aciraṁ vatayaṁ kāyo, pathaviṁ adhisessati; Chuddho apetaviññāṇo, niratthaṁva kaliṅgaraṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: body-layers, death-dying_

terms: [kāya](../terms/kaya.md), [viññāṇa](../terms/vinnana.md)

### 42 <a id="tea-dhammapada-42"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whatever an enemy might do to an enemy, or one who hates to one he hates, a wrongly directed mind (micchāpaṇihita citta) would do to one something worse than that.

> Nandagopālakavatthu Diso disaṁ yaṁ taṁ kayirā, verī vā pana verinaṁ; Micchāpaṇihitaṁ cittaṁ, pāpiyo naṁ tato kare.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [vera](../terms/vera.md), [paṇidhi](../terms/panidhi.md)

### 43 <a id="tea-dhammapada-43"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not a mother, a father, nor any other relatives could do for one what a rightly directed mind (sammāpaṇihita citta) would do: it makes one better off than that.

> Soreyyavatthu Na taṁ mātā pitā kayirā, aññe vāpi ca ñātakā; Sammāpaṇihitaṁ cittaṁ, seyyaso naṁ tato kare. Cittavaggo tatiyo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [paṇidhi](../terms/panidhi.md)

### 44 <a id="tea-dhammapada-44"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Who will search out (vicessati) this earth and Yama's world with its gods? Who will gather the well-taught word of the Dhamma (dhammapada sudesita), as a skilled person gathers a flower?

> Ko imaṁ pathaviṁ vicessati, Yamalokañca imaṁ sadevakaṁ; Ko dhammapadaṁ sudesitaṁ, Kusalo pupphamiva pacessati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: knowledge · stage: all · types: teacher-transmission, practice_

terms: [dhammapada](../terms/dhammapada.md), [yama](../terms/yama-pali.md)

### 45 <a id="tea-dhammapada-45"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

A trainee (sekha) will search out this earth and Yama's world with its gods; a trainee will gather the well-taught word of the Dhamma, as a skilled person gathers a flower.

> Sekho pathaviṁ vicessati, Yamalokañca imaṁ sadevakaṁ; Sekho dhammapadaṁ sudesitaṁ, Kusalo pupphamiva pacessati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: knowledge · stage: intermediate (sekha) · types: teacher-transmission, practice_

terms: [sekha](../terms/sekha.md), [dhammapada](../terms/dhammapada.md), [yama](../terms/yama-pali.md)

### 46 <a id="tea-dhammapada-46"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Knowing this body to be like foam (pheṇūpama), fully awake to its nature as a mirage (marīcidhamma), having cut off the flower-shafts (papupphaka) of Māra, one should go where the King of Death (maccurāja) cannot see.

> Marīcikammaṭṭhānikabhikkhuvatthu Pheṇūpamaṁ kāyamimaṁ viditvā, Marīcidhammaṁ abhisambudhāno; Chetvāna mārassa papupphakāni, Adassanaṁ maccurājassa gacche.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: intermediate · types: body-layers, practice_

terms: [kāya](../terms/kaya.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [maccu](../terms/maccu.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 47 <a id="tea-dhammapada-47"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Death carries off the man who is gathering flowers, his mind attached (byāsattamanasa), as a great flood carries off a sleeping village.

> Viṭaṭūbhavatthu Pupphāni heva pacinantaṁ, byāsattamanasaṁ naraṁ; Suttaṁ gāmaṁ mahoghova, maccu ādāya gacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying, ethics_

terms: [maccu](../terms/maccu.md)

### 48 <a id="tea-dhammapada-48"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The Ender (antaka) gets the man under his power who is gathering flowers, his mind attached, and is not yet satisfied with sense pleasures (kāmesu atitta).

> Patipūjikakumārivatthu Pupphāni heva pacinantaṁ, byāsattamanasaṁ naraṁ; Atittaññeva kāmesu, antako kurute vasaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying, ethics_

terms: [antaka](../terms/antaka.md), [kāma](../terms/kama.md)

### 49 <a id="tea-dhammapada-49"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a bee takes the nectar without harming the flower's colour or scent and goes on, so should the sage (muni) go about in the village.

> Macchariyakosiyaseṭṭhivatthu Yathāpi bhamaro pupphaṁ, vaṇṇagandhamaheṭhayaṁ; Paleti rasamādāya, evaṁ gāme munī care.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [muni](../terms/muni.md)

### 50 <a id="tea-dhammapada-50"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One should not look for the faults of others, nor at what others have done and left undone; one should look at one's own deeds done and undone.

> Pāveyyaājīvakavatthu Na paresaṁ vilomāni, na paresaṁ katākataṁ; Attanova avekkheyya, katāni akatāni ca.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

### 51 <a id="tea-dhammapada-51"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a lovely flower, colourful but without scent, so well-spoken speech is fruitless for one who does not act on it.

> Chattapāṇiupāsakavatthu Yathāpi ruciraṁ pupphaṁ, vaṇṇavantaṁ agandhakaṁ; Evaṁ subhāsitā vācā, aphalā hoti akubbato.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [vācā](../terms/vaca.md)

### 52 <a id="tea-dhammapada-52"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a lovely flower, colourful and scented, so well-spoken speech is fruitful for one who acts on it.

> Yathāpi ruciraṁ pupphaṁ, Vaṇṇavantaṁ sagandhakaṁ; Evaṁ subhāsitā vācā, Saphalā hoti kubbato.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [vācā](../terms/vaca.md)

### 53 <a id="tea-dhammapada-53"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As from a heap of flowers one could make many garlands, so a mortal (macca) who has been born should do much that is wholesome (kusala).

> Visākhāvatthu Yathāpi puppharāsimhā, kayirā mālāguṇe bahū; Evaṁ jātena maccena, kattabbaṁ kusalaṁ bahuṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [kusala](../terms/kusala.md), [macca](../terms/macca.md)

### 54 <a id="tea-dhammapada-54"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The scent of flowers does not go against the wind, nor that of sandalwood, tagara or jasmine; but the scent of the good (sataṁ) goes against the wind: the good person (sappurisa) pervades every direction.

> Ānandattherapañhavatthu Na pupphagandho paṭivātameti, Na candanaṁ tagaramallikā vā; Satañca gandho paṭivātameti, Sabbā disā sappuriso pavāyati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sappurisa](../terms/sappurisa.md)

### 55 <a id="tea-dhammapada-55"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Of sandalwood, tagara, lotus and vassikī, among these kinds of scent, the scent of virtue (sīlagandha) is unsurpassed.

> Candanaṁ tagaraṁ vāpi, uppalaṁ atha vassikī; Etesaṁ gandhajātānaṁ, sīlagandho anuttaro.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sīla](../terms/sila.md) · concepts: [Virtue (sīla)](../concepts/sila.md)

### 56 <a id="tea-dhammapada-56"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Slight is this scent of tagara and sandalwood; but the scent of the virtuous (sīlavant) wafts up among the gods as the best.

> Mahākassapattherapiṇḍapātadinnavatthu Appamatto ayaṁ gandho, yāyaṁ tagaracandanī; Yo ca sīlavataṁ gandho, vāti devesu uttamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, world-fate_

terms: [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md) · concepts: [Virtue (sīla)](../concepts/sila.md)

### 57 <a id="tea-dhammapada-57"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Of those who are perfect in virtue (sampannasīla), who dwell in heedfulness (appamādavihārī), freed by right knowledge (sammadaññāvimutta), Māra does not find the path.

> Godhikattheraparinibbānavatthu Tesaṁ sampannasīlānaṁ, appamādavihārinaṁ; Sammadaññāvimuttānaṁ, māro maggaṁ na vindati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: realized · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [vimutti](../terms/vimutti.md) · practices: [Heedfulness (appamāda)](../practices/appamada.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 58 <a id="tea-dhammapada-58"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As on a heap of rubbish thrown on the great highway a lotus might grow, fragrant and delightful,

> Garahadinnavatthu Yathā saṅkāradhānasmiṁ, ujjhitasmiṁ mahāpathe; Padumaṁ tattha jāyetha, sucigandhaṁ manoramaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

### 59 <a id="tea-dhammapada-59"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

So among those who are like rubbish, among the blind ordinary people (puthujjana), the disciple of the perfectly self-awakened one (sammāsambuddhasāvaka) outshines them by wisdom (paññā).

> Evaṁ saṅkārabhūtesu, andhabhūte puthujjane; Atirocati paññāya, sammāsambuddhasāvako. Pupphavaggo catuttho.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: knowledge · stage: intermediate · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [puthujjana](../terms/puthujjana.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [sāvaka](../terms/savaka.md), [sammāsambuddha](../terms/sammasambuddha.md)

### 60 <a id="tea-dhammapada-60"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Long is the night for one who is awake; long is the league (yojana) for one who is weary; long is saṃsāra for fools (bāla) who do not know the true Dhamma (saddhamma).

> Dīghā jāgarato ratti, dīghaṁ santassa yojanaṁ; Dīgho bālāna saṁsāro, saddhammaṁ avijānataṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, consciousness-mind_

terms: [saṃsāra](../terms/samsara.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [saddhamma](../terms/saddhamma.md) · concepts: [Saṃsāra](../concepts/samsara.md)

### 61 <a id="tea-dhammapada-61"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If, faring on, one does not find a companion better than or equal to oneself, one should firmly keep to a solitary life: there is no companionship with a fool.

> Mahākassapasaddhivihārikavatthu Carañce nādhigaccheyya, Seyyaṁ sadisamattano; Ekacariyaṁ daḷhaṁ kayirā, Natthi bāle sahāyatā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md)

### 62 <a id="tea-dhammapada-62"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

'I have sons, I have wealth': so the fool is troubled. For even oneself (attā) is not one's own (attā hi attano natthi): how then sons, how wealth?

> Ānandaseṭṭhivatthu Puttā matthi dhanaṁ matthi, iti bālo vihaññati; Attā hi attano natthi, kuto puttā kuto dhanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [attā](../terms/atta.md)

### 63 <a id="tea-dhammapada-63"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

A fool who knows his foolishness (bālya) is by that much wise; but a fool who thinks himself wise (paṇḍitamānī) is the one who is rightly called a fool.

> Gaṇṭhibhedakacoravatthu Yo bālo maññati bālyaṁ, paṇḍito vāpi tena so; Bālo ca paṇḍitamānī, sa ve “bālo”ti vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, ethics_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [māna](../terms/mana.md) · obstacles: [Conceit (māna)](../obstacles/mana.md)

### 64 <a id="tea-dhammapada-64"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even if a fool attends on a wise person all his life, he does not know the Dhamma, as a spoon does not know the taste of the soup.

> Udāyittheravatthu Yāvajīvampi ce bālo, paṇḍitaṁ payirupāsati; Na so dhammaṁ vijānāti, dabbī sūparasaṁ yathā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, teacher-transmission_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md)

### 65 <a id="tea-dhammapada-65"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

But if an understanding person (viññū) attends on a wise person even for a moment, he quickly knows the Dhamma, as the tongue knows the taste of the soup.

> Tiṁsapāveyyakabhikkhuvatthu Muhuttamapi ce viññū, paṇḍitaṁ payirupāsati; Khippaṁ dhammaṁ vijānāti, jivhā sūparasaṁ yathā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, teacher-transmission_

terms: [viññū](../terms/vinnu.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md)

### 66 <a id="tea-dhammapada-66"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Fools of poor wisdom go about with themselves as their own enemy (amitteneva attanā), doing the evil deed that has a bitter fruit.

> Suppabuddhakuṭṭhivatthu Caranti bālā dummedhā, amitteneva attanā; Karontā pāpakaṁ kammaṁ, yaṁ hoti kaṭukapphalaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, ethics_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [kamma](../terms/kamma.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md)

### 67 <a id="tea-dhammapada-67"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

That deed is not well done which one regrets after doing it, whose result one experiences weeping with a tearful face.

> Kassakavatthu Na taṁ kammaṁ kataṁ sādhu, yaṁ katvā anutappati; Yassa assumukho rodaṁ, vipākaṁ paṭisevati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, ethics_

terms: [kamma](../terms/kamma.md), [vipāka](../terms/vipaka.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md)

### 68 <a id="tea-dhammapada-68"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

That deed is well done which one does not regret after doing it, whose result one experiences glad and cheerful.

> Sumanamālākāravatthu Tañca kammaṁ kataṁ sādhu, yaṁ katvā nānutappati; Yassa patīto sumano, vipākaṁ paṭisevati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, ethics_

terms: [kamma](../terms/kamma.md), [vipāka](../terms/vipaka.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md)

### 69 <a id="tea-dhammapada-69"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The fool thinks evil is like honey as long as it has not ripened; but when the evil ripens, then he comes to suffering (dukkha).

> Uppalavaṇṇattherīvatthu Madhuṁvā maññati bālo, yāva pāpaṁ na paccati; Yadā ca paccati pāpaṁ, atha dukkhaṁ nigacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 70 <a id="tea-dhammapada-70"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even if a fool ate his food month after month with the tip of a blade of kusa grass, he is not worth a sixteenth part of those who have understood the Dhamma (saṅkhātadhammā).

> Jambukattheravatthu Māse māse kusaggena, bālo bhuñjeyya bhojanaṁ; Na so saṅkhātadhammānaṁ, kalaṁ agghati soḷasiṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [tapas](../terms/tapas.md) · practices: [Naked ascetic observances (acelaka-vata)](../practices/acelaka-vata.md) · obstacles: [Austerity of the fool (bāla-tapas)](../obstacles/bala-tapa.md)

### 71 <a id="tea-dhammapada-71"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

An evil deed, once done, does not turn at once, like fresh milk (that curdles); smouldering, it follows the fool, like fire covered with ash.

> Ahipetavatthu Na hi pāpaṁ kataṁ kammaṁ, Sajjukhīraṁva muccati; Ḍahantaṁ bālamanveti, Bhasmacchannova pāvako.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [kamma](../terms/kamma.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md)

### 72 <a id="tea-dhammapada-72"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Whatever knowledge/notoriety (ñatta) arises for a fool is only for his harm: it destroys the bright part (sukkaṁsa) of the fool and splits his head.

> Saṭṭhikūṭapetavatthu Yāvadeva anatthāya, ñattaṁ bālassa jāyati; Hanti bālassa sukkaṁsaṁ, muddhamassa vipātayaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md)

### 73 <a id="tea-dhammapada-73"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

He would wish for unreal (asanta) standing, for precedence among the bhikkhus, for lordship in monasteries and for veneration among other families.

> Cittagahapativatthu Asantaṁ bhāvanamiccheyya, Purekkhārañca bhikkhusu; Āvāsesu ca issariyaṁ, Pūjaṁ parakulesu ca.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

### 74 <a id="tea-dhammapada-74"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'Let both householders and renunciants think: this was done by me alone; let them be subject to me in whatever is to be done or not done': such is the fool's intention (saṅkappa), and his desire (icchā) and conceit (māna) increase.

> Mameva kata maññantu, gihī pabbajitā ubho; Mamevātivasā assu, kiccākiccesu kismici; Iti bālassa saṅkappo, icchā māno ca vaḍḍhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [icchā](../terms/iccha-pali.md), [māna](../terms/mana.md), [saṅkappa](../terms/sankappa.md) · obstacles: [Conceit (māna)](../obstacles/mana.md)

### 75 <a id="tea-dhammapada-75"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One is the path to gain (lābhūpanisā), another the path that leads to nibbāna: knowing this, the bhikkhu, the Buddha's disciple, should not delight in honour (sakkāra) but should foster seclusion (viveka).

> Vanavāsitissasāmaṇeravatthu Aññā hi lābhūpanisā, aññā nibbānagāminī; Evametaṁ abhiññāya, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvako; Sakkāraṁ nābhinandeyya, vivekamanubrūhaye. Bālavaggo pañcamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [lābha](../terms/labha-pali.md), [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md), [sakkāra](../terms/sakkara.md), [viveka](../terms/viveka.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md) · concepts: [Nibbāna](../concepts/nibbana.md) · practices: [Seclusion (viveka)](../practices/viveka.md)

### 76 <a id="tea-dhammapada-76"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should attend on such a wise person as points out one's faults (vajjadassī), a reprover (niggayhavādī), as if pointing out hidden treasures; for one who attends on such a person it becomes better, not worse.

> Nidhīnaṁva pavattāraṁ, yaṁ passe vajjadassinaṁ; Niggayhavādiṁ medhāviṁ, tādisaṁ paṇḍitaṁ bhaje; Tādisaṁ bhajamānassa, seyyo hoti na pāpiyo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, teacher-transmission_

terms: [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md)

### 77 <a id="tea-dhammapada-77"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should advise and instruct and restrain from what is improper (asabbhā); for such a one is dear to the good and disliked by the bad.

> Assajipunabbasukavatthu Ovadeyyānusāseyya, asabbhā ca nivāraye; Satañhi so piyo hoti, asataṁ hoti appiyo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

### 78 <a id="tea-dhammapada-78"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Do not keep company with bad friends (pāpaka mitta), nor with the lowest of men; keep company with good friends (kalyāṇamitta) and with the best of men.

> Channattheravatthu Na bhaje pāpake mitte, na bhaje purisādhame; Bhajetha mitte kalyāṇe, bhajetha purisuttame.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [kalyāṇamitta](../terms/kalyanamitta.md), [pāpamitta](../terms/papamitta.md) · concepts: [Good friendship](../concepts/kalyanamittata.md)

### 79 <a id="tea-dhammapada-79"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One who has joy in the Dhamma (dhammapīti) lies at ease (sukhaṁ seti) with a clear, serene mind; the wise always delight in the Dhamma proclaimed by the noble ones (ariya).

> Mahākappinattheravatthu Dhammapīti sukhaṁ seti, vippasannena cetasā; Ariyappavedite dhamme, sadā ramati paṇḍito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [ariya](../terms/ariya.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md)

### 80 <a id="tea-dhammapada-80"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Irrigators lead water, fletchers bend the arrow, carpenters bend timber; the wise tame themselves (attānaṁ damayanti).

> Paṇḍitasāmaṇeravatthu Udakañhi nayanti nettikā, Usukārā namayanti tejanaṁ; Dāruṁ namayanti tacchakā, Attānaṁ damayanti paṇḍitā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md) · practices: [Taming oneself (attadamana)](../practices/atta-damana.md)

### 81 <a id="tea-dhammapada-81"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a solid rock is not shaken by the wind, so the wise are not moved by blame and praise (nindāpasaṁsā).

> Lakuṇḍakabhaddiyattheravatthu Selo yathā ekaghano, vātena na samīrati; Evaṁ nindāpasaṁsāsu, na samiñjanti paṇḍitā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: advanced · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md)

### 82 <a id="tea-dhammapada-82"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Like a deep lake, clear and undisturbed, so the wise become clear on hearing the teachings (dhammāni).

> Kāṇamātāvatthu Yathāpi rahado gambhīro, vippasanno anāvilo; Evaṁ dhammāni sutvāna, vippasīdanti paṇḍitā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md)

### 83 <a id="tea-dhammapada-83"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

The good (sappurisā) let go everywhere; the tranquil do not chatter out of desire for sense pleasures; touched by pleasure or by pain the wise show neither exaltation nor depression (na uccāvacaṁ).

> Pañcasatabhikkhuvatthu Sabbattha ve sappurisā cajanti, Na kāmakāmā lapayanti santo; Sukhena phuṭṭhā atha vā dukhena, Na uccāvacaṁ paṇḍitā dassayanti.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: advanced · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [sappurisa](../terms/sappurisa.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md), [kāma](../terms/kama.md)

### 84 <a id="tea-dhammapada-84"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Neither for one's own sake nor for another's should one wish for sons, wealth or a kingdom, nor wish for one's own success by unrighteous means (adhamma); such a one would be virtuous (sīlavā), wise (paññavā) and righteous (dhammika).

> Dhammikattheravatthu Na attahetu na parassa hetu, Na puttamicche na dhanaṁ na raṭṭhaṁ; Na iccheyya adhammena samiddhimattano, Sa sīlavā paññavā dhammiko siyā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [dhammika](../terms/dhammika.md), [adhamma](../terms/adhamma.md) · concepts: [Virtue (sīla)](../concepts/sila.md)

### 85 <a id="tea-dhammapada-85"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Few among human beings are those who go to the far shore (pāragāmī); the rest of the people run up and down only along the near bank.

> Dhammassavanattheravatthu Appakā te manussesu, ye janā pāragāmino; Athāyaṁ itarā pajā, tīramevānudhāvati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāragāmī](../terms/paragami.md)

### 86 <a id="tea-dhammapada-86"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

But those who follow the Dhamma when the Dhamma is well taught will cross the realm of death (maccudheyya), so hard to cross.

> Ye ca kho sammadakkhāte, dhamme dhammānuvattino; Te janā pāramessanti, maccudheyyaṁ suduttaraṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, practice_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [maccu](../terms/maccu.md)

### 87 <a id="tea-dhammapada-87"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Having abandoned the dark quality (kaṇha dhamma), the wise one should develop the bright (sukka): coming from home to homelessness (okā anokam), in seclusion (viveka) where delight is hard to find.

> Pañcasataāgantukabhikkhuvatthu Kaṇhaṁ dhammaṁ vippahāya, Sukkaṁ bhāvetha paṇḍito; Okā anokamāgamma, Viveke yattha dūramaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [kaṇha](../terms/kanha.md), [sukka](../terms/sukka.md), [viveka](../terms/viveka.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md) · practices: [Seclusion (viveka)](../practices/viveka.md)

### 88 <a id="tea-dhammapada-88"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

There he should seek delight, having abandoned sense pleasures (kāma), owning nothing (akiñcana); the wise one should cleanse himself of the defilements of the mind (cittakilesa).

> Tatrābhiratimiccheyya, hitvā kāme akiñcano; Pariyodapeyya attānaṁ, cittaklesehi paṇḍito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [kāma](../terms/kama.md), [akiñcana](../terms/akincana.md), [citta](../terms/citta.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md)

### 89 <a id="tea-dhammapada-89"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Those whose minds are rightly well developed in the factors of awakening (sambojjhaṅga), who delight in relinquishing (ādānapaṭinissagga) without clinging (anupādāya), with cankers destroyed (khīṇāsava), radiant (jutimanta), are completely extinguished (parinibbuta) in the world.

> Yesaṁ sambodhiyaṅgesu, sammā cittaṁ subhāvitaṁ; Ādānapaṭinissagge, anupādāya ye ratā; Khīṇāsavā jutimanto, te loke parinibbutā. Paṇḍitavaggo chaṭṭho.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: meditation · stage: realized · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [sambojjhaṅga](../terms/sambojjhanga.md), [citta](../terms/citta.md), [āsava](../terms/asava.md), [parinibbāna](../terms/parinibbana.md)

### 90 <a id="tea-dhammapada-90"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For one who has completed the journey (gataddhin), is free from sorrow, freed everywhere, with all ties (gantha) abandoned, no fever (pariḷāha) is found.

> Gataddhino visokassa, vippamuttassa sabbadhi; Sabbaganthappahīnassa, pariḷāho na vijjati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [gantha](../terms/gantha.md), [pariḷāha](../terms/parilaha.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md) · obstacles: [Grief (soka)](../obstacles/soka-pali.md)

### 91 <a id="tea-dhammapada-91"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The mindful (satīmanta) exert themselves; they take no delight in a dwelling (nikete); like swans leaving a pond, they abandon house after house.

> Mahākassapattheravatthu Uyyuñjanti satīmanto, na nikete ramanti te; Haṁsāva pallalaṁ hitvā, okamokaṁ jahanti te.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [sati](../terms/sati.md)

### 92 <a id="tea-dhammapada-92"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Those with no hoarding (sannicaya), who have fully understood food (pariññātabhojana), whose range (gocara) is the liberation (vimokkha) that is empty (suññata) and signless (animitta): their course is hard to trace, like that of birds in the sky.

> Belaṭṭhasīsattheravatthu Yesaṁ sannicayo natthi, ye pariññātabhojanā; Suññato animitto ca, vimokkho yesaṁ gocaro; Ākāseva sakuntānaṁ, gati tesaṁ durannayā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [vimokkha](../terms/vimokkha.md), [suññatā](../terms/sunnata.md), [animitta](../terms/animitta.md)

### 93 <a id="tea-dhammapada-93"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One whose cankers (āsava) are destroyed, who is not dependent on food, whose range is the liberation that is empty and signless: his track is hard to trace, like that of birds in the sky.

> Anuruddhattheravatthu Yassāsavā parikkhīṇā, āhāre ca anissito; Suññato animitto ca, vimokkho yassa gocaro; Ākāseva sakuntānaṁ, padaṁ tassa durannayaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [āsava](../terms/asava.md), [vimokkha](../terms/vimokkha.md), [suññatā](../terms/sunnata.md), [animitta](../terms/animitta.md)

### 94 <a id="tea-dhammapada-94"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One whose faculties (indriya) have gone to calm (samatha), like horses well tamed by a charioteer, who has abandoned conceit (māna) and is without cankers (anāsava): even the gods envy such a one (tādī).

> Mahākaccāyanattheravatthu Yassindriyāni samathaṅgatāni, Assā yathā sārathinā sudantā; Pahīnamānassa anāsavassa, Devāpi tassa pihayanti tādino.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [indriya](../terms/indriya.md), [samatha](../terms/samatha.md), [māna](../terms/mana.md), [āsava](../terms/asava.md), [tādī](../terms/tadi.md), [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md) · obstacles: [Conceit (māna)](../obstacles/mana.md)

### 95 <a id="tea-dhammapada-95"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Like the earth, such a one (tādī) does not resent; firm in vows (subbata) like an Indra's pillar; like a lake without mud: for such a one the rounds of saṃsāra do not occur.

> Sāriputtattheravatthu Pathavisamo no virujjhati, Indakhilupamo tādi subbato; Rahadova apetakaddamo, Saṁsārā na bhavanti tādino.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [tādī](../terms/tadi.md), [saṃsāra](../terms/samsara.md) · concepts: [Saṃsāra](../concepts/samsara.md)

### 96 <a id="tea-dhammapada-96"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Peaceful is his mind, peaceful his speech and deed, of one freed by right knowledge (sammadaññāvimutta), one who is calmed (upasanta), such a one (tādī).

> Kosambivāsitissattherasāmaṇeravatthu Santaṁ tassa manaṁ hoti, santā vācā ca kamma ca; Sammadaññāvimuttassa, upasantassa tādino.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [tādī](../terms/tadi.md), [upasama](../terms/upasama.md), [vimutti](../terms/vimutti.md)

### 97 <a id="tea-dhammapada-97"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

The person who is without faith (assaddha), ungrateful (akataññū), a house-breaker (sandhiccheda), whose opportunity is destroyed (hatāvakāsa), who has vomited out hopes (vantāsa): he indeed is the supreme person (uttamaporisa). Taken in their ordinary senses the words are terms of censure, yet the verse calls such a person supreme; it does not itself say how the words are to be taken.

> Sāriputtattheravatthu Assaddho akataññū ca, sandhicchedo ca yo naro; Hatāvakāso vantāso, sa ve uttamaporiso.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [assaddha](../terms/assaddha.md), [akataññū](../terms/akatannu.md), [sandhicchedin](../terms/sandhiccheda.md), [uttamaporisa](../terms/uttamaporisa.md)

### 98 <a id="tea-dhammapada-98"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

In village or in forest, in valley or on high ground: wherever arahants dwell, that ground is delightful (rāmaṇeyyaka).

> Khadiravaniyarevatattheravatthu Gāme vā yadi vāraññe, Ninne vā yadi vā thale; Yattha arahanto viharanti, Taṁ bhūmirāmaṇeyyakaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [arahant](../terms/arahant.md)

### 99 <a id="tea-dhammapada-99"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Delightful are the forests where the crowd does not delight; the passionless (vītarāga) will delight there, not those who seek sense pleasures (kāmagavesin).

> Aññataraitthivatthu Ramaṇīyāni araññāni, yattha na ramatī jano; Vītarāgā ramissanti, na te kāmagavesino. Arahantavaggo sattamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized (arahant) · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

### 100 <a id="tea-dhammapada-100"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even a thousand utterances (vācā) made up of meaningless words are surpassed by one meaningful word (atthapada) on hearing which one is calmed (upasammati).

> Sahassamapi ce vācā, anatthapadasaṁhitā; Ekaṁ atthapadaṁ seyyo, yaṁ sutvā upasammati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: sound-language, practice_

### 101 <a id="tea-dhammapada-101"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even a thousand verses (gāthā) made up of meaningless words are surpassed by one line of verse (gāthāpada) on hearing which one is calmed.

> Bāhiyadārucīriyattheravatthu Sahassamapi ce gāthā, anatthapadasaṁhitā; Ekaṁ gāthāpadaṁ seyyo, yaṁ sutvā upasammati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: sound-language, practice_

terms: [upasama](../terms/upasama.md)

### 102 <a id="tea-dhammapada-102"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better than reciting a hundred verses made up of meaningless words is one Dhamma-word (dhammapada), on hearing which one is calmed.

> Kuṇḍalakesitherīvatthu Yo ca gāthā sataṁ bhāse, anatthapadasaṁhitā; Ekaṁ dhammapadaṁ seyyo, yaṁ sutvā upasammati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: sound-language, practice_
