### 103 <a id="tea-dhammapada-103"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whoever in battle should conquer a thousand times a thousand men, but the one who conquers just one thing, oneself (attā): that one is indeed the best of victors in battle.

> Yo sahassaṁ sahassena, saṅgāme mānuse jine; Ekañca jeyyamattānaṁ, sa ve saṅgāmajuttamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, karma-liberation, ethics_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md)

### 104 <a id="tea-dhammapada-104"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better is oneself conquered than these other people, for a person who is self-tamed (attadanta) and constantly lives restrained (saññatacārī).

> Anatthapucchakabrāhmaṇavatthu Attā have jitaṁ seyyo, yā cāyaṁ itarā pajā; Attadantassa posassa, niccaṁ saññatacārino.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md)

### 105 <a id="tea-dhammapada-105"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Neither a god (deva), nor a gandhabba, nor Māra together with Brahmā, could turn into defeat the victory of one of such a kind.

> Neva devo na gandhabbo, na māro saha brahmunā; Jitaṁ apajitaṁ kayirā, tathārūpassa jantuno.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 106 <a id="tea-dhammapada-106"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One who month after month for a hundred years should sacrifice with a thousand (offerings), or one who should for a moment honour a single person of developed self (bhāvitatta): that honouring is better than the hundred years of offering.

> Sāriputtattherassa mātulabrāhmaṇavatthu Māse māse sahassena, yo yajetha sataṁ samaṁ; Ekañca bhāvitattānaṁ, muhuttamapi pūjaye; Sāyeva pūjanā seyyo, yañce vassasataṁ hutaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: polemical · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [yajati](../terms/yaja.md), [bhāvitatta](../terms/bhavitatta.md)

### 107 <a id="tea-dhammapada-107"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One who for a hundred years should tend the (sacrificial) fire in the forest, or one who should for a moment honour a single person of developed self: that honouring is better than the hundred years of offering.

> Sāriputtattherassa bhāgineyyavatthu Yo ca vassasataṁ jantu, aggiṁ paricare vane; Ekañca bhāvitattānaṁ, muhuttamapi pūjaye; Sāyeva pūjanā seyyo, yañce vassasataṁ hutaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: polemical · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [bhāvitatta](../terms/bhavitatta.md)

### 108 <a id="tea-dhammapada-108"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Whatever is sacrificed or offered in the world for a year by one seeking merit (puññapekkha), all of it does not amount to a quarter; paying respect (abhivādanā) to the upright (ujjugata) is better.

> Sāriputtatherassa sahāyakabrāhmaṇavatthu Yaṁ kiñci yiṭṭhaṁ va hutaṁ va loke, Saṁvaccharaṁ yajetha puññapekkho; Sabbampi taṁ na catubhāgameti, Abhivādanā ujjugatesu seyyo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: polemical · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [abhivādana](../terms/abhivadana.md)

### 109 <a id="tea-dhammapada-109"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For one who is in the habit of paying respect and constantly honours elders, four things increase: life-span (āyu), beauty/complexion (vaṇṇa), happiness (sukha) and strength (bala).

> Āyuvaḍḍhanakumāravatthu Abhivādanasīlissa, niccaṁ vuḍḍhāpacāyino; Cattāro dhammā vaḍḍhanti, āyu vaṇṇo sukhaṁ balaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [āyu](../terms/ayu-lifespan.md), [vaṇṇa](../terms/vanna-beauty.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [bala](../terms/bala-strength.md)

### 110 <a id="tea-dhammapada-110"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better one day of life for a virtuous (sīlavant) meditator (jhāyī) than a hundred years' life of one who is immoral (dussīla) and unconcentrated (asamāhita).

> Saṅkiccasāmaṇeravatthu Yo ca vassasataṁ jīve, dussīlo asamāhito; Ekāhaṁ jīvitaṁ seyyo, sīlavantassa jhāyino.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md), [jhāna](../terms/jhana.md)

### 111 <a id="tea-dhammapada-111"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better one day of life for a wise (paññavant) meditator than a hundred years' life of one who is unwise (duppañña) and unconcentrated.

> Khāṇukoṇḍaññattheravatthu Yo ca vassasataṁ jīve, duppañño asamāhito; Ekāhaṁ jīvitaṁ seyyo, paññavantassa jhāyino.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md), [jhāna](../terms/jhana.md)

### 112 <a id="tea-dhammapada-112"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better one day of life for one who firmly arouses energy (vīriya) than a hundred years' life of one who is lazy (kusīta) and of poor energy.

> Sabbadāsattheravatthu Yo ca vassasataṁ jīve, kusīto hīnavīriyo; Ekāhaṁ jīvitaṁ seyyo, vīriyamārabhato daḷhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [viriya](../terms/viriya.md)

### 113 <a id="tea-dhammapada-113"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better one day of life for one who sees rise and fall (udayabbaya) than a hundred years' life of one who does not see rise and fall.

> Paṭācārātherīvatthu Yo ca vassasataṁ jīve, apassaṁ udayabbayaṁ; Ekāhaṁ jīvitaṁ seyyo, passato udayabbayaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [udayabbaya](../terms/udayabbaya.md)

### 114 <a id="tea-dhammapada-114"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better one day of life for one who sees the deathless state (amataṁ padaṁ) than a hundred years' life of one who does not see the deathless state.

> Kisāgotamīvatthu Yo ca vassasataṁ jīve, apassaṁ amataṁ padaṁ; Ekāhaṁ jīvitaṁ seyyo, passato amataṁ padaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ultimate_

terms: [amata](../terms/amata.md)

### 115 <a id="tea-dhammapada-115"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better one day of life for one who sees the supreme Dhamma (dhammamuttamaṁ) than a hundred years' life of one who does not see the supreme Dhamma.

> Bahuputtikattherīvatthu Yo ca vassasataṁ jīve, apassaṁ dhammamuttamaṁ; Ekāhaṁ jīvitaṁ seyyo, passato dhammamuttamaṁ. Sahassavaggo aṭṭhamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ultimate_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md)

### 116 <a id="tea-dhammapada-116"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should hasten to the good (kalyāṇa) and hold the mind back from evil (pāpa); for one who is slow in doing merit (puñña), the mind delights in evil.

> Abhittharetha kalyāṇe, pāpā cittaṁ nivāraye; Dandhañhi karoto puññaṁ, pāpasmiṁ ramatī mano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

terms: [kalyāṇa](../terms/kalyana.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [mano](../terms/mano.md), [citta](../terms/citta.md)

### 117 <a id="tea-dhammapada-117"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If a person does evil (pāpa), he should not do it again and again, nor set his wish (chanda) on it: the accumulation of evil is painful (dukkha).

> Seyyasakattheravatthu Pāpañce puriso kayirā, Na naṁ kayirā punappunaṁ; Na tamhi chandaṁ kayirātha, Dukkho pāpassa uccayo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [chanda](../terms/chanda.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 118 <a id="tea-dhammapada-118"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If a person does merit (puñña), he should do it again and again and set his wish (chanda) on it: the accumulation of merit is happiness (sukha).

> Lājadevadhītāvatthu Puññañce puriso kayirā, kayirā naṁ punappunaṁ; Tamhi chandaṁ kayirātha, sukho puññassa uccayo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [chanda](../terms/chanda.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 119 <a id="tea-dhammapada-119"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even the evil person sees good fortune as long as the evil has not ripened; but when the evil ripens, then the evil person sees evil results.

> Anāthapiṇḍikaseṭṭhivatthu Pāpopi passati bhadraṁ, Yāva pāpaṁ na paccati; Yadā ca paccati pāpaṁ, Atha pāpo pāpāni passati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md)

### 120 <a id="tea-dhammapada-120"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even the good person sees evil as long as the good has not ripened; but when the good ripens, then the good person sees good results.

> Bhadropi passati pāpaṁ, Yāva bhadraṁ na paccati; Yadā ca paccati bhadraṁ, Atha bhadro bhadrāni passati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md)

### 121 <a id="tea-dhammapada-121"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Do not think lightly of evil, saying 'it will not come to me'; even a water-jar is filled by falling drops of water; the fool is filled with evil, gathering it little by little.

> Asaññataparikkhāravatthu Māvamaññetha pāpassa, na mantaṁ āgamissati; Udabindunipātena, udakumbhopi pūrati; Bālo pūrati pāpassa, thokaṁ thokampi ācinaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md)

### 122 <a id="tea-dhammapada-122"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Do not think lightly of merit, saying 'it will not come to me'; even a water-jar is filled by falling drops of water; the steadfast one (dhīra) is filled with merit, gathering it little by little.

> Biḷālapādakaseṭṭhivatthu Māvamaññetha puññassa, na mandaṁ āgamissati; Udabindunipātena, udakumbhopi pūrati; Dhīro pūrati puññassa, thokaṁ thokampi ācinaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [dhīra](../terms/dhira-pali.md)

### 123 <a id="tea-dhammapada-123"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a merchant with a small escort and great wealth avoids a dangerous road, and as one who wants to live avoids poison, so one should avoid evil deeds (pāpāni).

> Mahādhanavāṇijavatthu Vāṇijova bhayaṁ maggaṁ, appasattho mahaddhano; Visaṁ jīvitukāmova, pāpāni parivajjaye.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md)

### 124 <a id="tea-dhammapada-124"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If there is no wound on the hand, one could carry poison in the hand; poison does not affect one without a wound; there is no evil for one who does not do it.

> Kukkuṭamittanesādavatthu Pāṇimhi ce vaṇo nāssa, hareyya pāṇinā visaṁ; Nābbaṇaṁ visamanveti, natthi pāpaṁ akubbato.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md)

### 125 <a id="tea-dhammapada-125"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whoever offends against an inoffensive person, one who is pure and blameless (anaṅgaṇa), that evil returns to that very fool, like fine dust thrown against the wind.

> Kokasunakhaluddakavatthu Yo appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati, Suddhassa posassa anaṅgaṇassa; Tameva bālaṁ pacceti pāpaṁ, Sukhumo rajo paṭivātaṁva khitto.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md)

### 126 <a id="tea-dhammapada-126"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Some are reborn in a womb; evildoers (pāpakammino) go to hell (niraya); those of good going (sugatino) go to heaven (sagga); the taintless (anāsava) attain complete extinguishment (parinibbāna).

> Maṇikārakulūpakatissattheravatthu Gabbhameke uppajjanti, nirayaṁ pāpakammino; Saggaṁ sugatino yanti, parinibbanti anāsavā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: cosmic · path: general · stage: all · types: world-fate, karma-liberation_

terms: [niraya](../terms/niraya.md), [sagga](../terms/sagga.md), [anāsava](../terms/anasava.md), [parinibbāna](../terms/parinibbana.md)

### 127 <a id="tea-dhammapada-127"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Neither in the sky, nor in the midst of the sea, nor by entering a mountain cleft is there any place on earth where one could stand and be freed from an evil deed (pāpakamma).

> Tayojanavatthu Na antalikkhe na samuddamajjhe, Na pabbatānaṁ vivaraṁ pavissa; Na vijjatī so jagatippadeso, Yatthaṭṭhito mucceyya pāpakammā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [kamma](../terms/kamma.md)

### 128 <a id="tea-dhammapada-128"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Neither in the sky, nor in the midst of the sea, nor by entering a mountain cleft is there any place on earth where one standing would not be overpowered by death (maccu).

> Suppabuddhasakyavatthu Na antalikkhe na samuddamajjhe, Na pabbatānaṁ vivaraṁ pavissa; Na vijjatī so jagatippadeso, Yatthaṭṭhitaṁ nappasaheyya maccu. Pāpavaggo navamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: cosmic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [maccu](../terms/maccu.md)

### 129 <a id="tea-dhammapada-129"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

All tremble at the rod (daṇḍa); all fear death (maccu). Making oneself the comparison, one should neither kill nor cause to kill.

> Sabbe tasanti daṇḍassa, sabbe bhāyanti maccuno; Attānaṁ upamaṁ katvā, na haneyya na ghātaye.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [daṇḍa](../terms/danda-pali.md), [maccu](../terms/maccu.md)

### 130 <a id="tea-dhammapada-130"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

All tremble at the rod; life is dear to all. Making oneself the comparison, one should neither kill nor cause to kill.

> Chabbaggiyavatthu Sabbe tasanti daṇḍassa, sabbesaṁ jīvitaṁ piyaṁ; Attānaṁ upamaṁ katvā, na haneyya na ghātaye.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [daṇḍa](../terms/danda-pali.md)

### 131 <a id="tea-dhammapada-131"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who harms with the rod beings who wish for happiness, while seeking happiness for himself, does not gain happiness after death (pecca).

> Sambahulakumārakavatthu Sukhakāmāni bhūtāni, yo daṇḍena vihiṁsati; Attano sukhamesāno, pecca so na labhate sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [daṇḍa](../terms/danda-pali.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 132 <a id="tea-dhammapada-132"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who does not harm with the rod beings who wish for happiness, while seeking happiness for himself, gains happiness after death.

> Sukhakāmāni bhūtāni, yo daṇḍena na hiṁsati; Attano sukhamesāno, pecca so labhate sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [daṇḍa](../terms/danda-pali.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 133 <a id="tea-dhammapada-133"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Do not speak harshly to anyone; those addressed may answer you back. Quarrelsome talk (sārambhakathā) is painful, and blows in return (paṭidaṇḍa) may touch you.

> Koṇḍadhānattheravatthu Māvoca pharusaṁ kañci, vuttā paṭivadeyyu taṁ; Dukkhā hi sārambhakathā, paṭidaṇḍā phuseyyu taṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sārambha](../terms/sarambha.md), [daṇḍa](../terms/danda-pali.md)

### 134 <a id="tea-dhammapada-134"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If you make yourself still (neresi attānaṁ), like a battered gong, then you have reached nibbāna: there is no quarrelsomeness (sārambha) in you.

> Sace neresi attānaṁ, kaṁso upahato yathā; Esa pattosi nibbānaṁ, sārambho te na vijjati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [sārambha](../terms/sarambha.md), [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md), [attā](../terms/atta.md)

### 135 <a id="tea-dhammapada-135"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a cowherd with a stick drives the cattle to pasture, so ageing (jarā) and death (maccu) drive on the life-span (āyu) of living beings.

> Uposathikaitthīnaṁvatthu Yathā daṇḍena gopālo, gāvo pājeti gocaraṁ; Evaṁ jarā ca maccu ca, āyuṁ pājenti pāṇinaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [jarā](../terms/jara.md), [maccu](../terms/maccu.md)

### 136 <a id="tea-dhammapada-136"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Doing evil deeds, the fool does not realise it (na bujjhati); the dull-witted one is tormented by his own deeds, as if burned by fire.

> Ajagarapetavatthu Atha pāpāni kammāni, karaṁ bālo na bujjhati; Sehi kammehi dummedho, aggidaḍḍhova tappati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md)

### 137 <a id="tea-dhammapada-137"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who harms with the rod the rodless, the unoffending, quickly comes to one of ten states.

> Mahāmoggallānattheravatthu Yo daṇḍena adaṇḍesu, appaduṭṭhesu dussati; Dasannamaññataraṁ ṭhānaṁ, khippameva nigacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [daṇḍa](../terms/danda-pali.md)

### 138 <a id="tea-dhammapada-138"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

(These are among the ten:) harsh pain (vedanā), loss (jāni), breaking up of the body, grave affliction (ābādha), or derangement of mind (cittakkhepa).

> Vedanaṁ pharusaṁ jāniṁ, sarīrassa va bhedanaṁ; Garukaṁ vāpi ābādhaṁ, cittakkhepaṁ va pāpuṇe.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [vedanā](../terms/vedana.md)

### 139 <a id="tea-dhammapada-139"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Or trouble from the ruler, cruel slander (abbhakkhāna), loss of relatives, or destruction of wealth.

> Rājato vā upasaggaṁ, Abbhakkhānaṁ va dāruṇaṁ; Parikkhayaṁ va ñātīnaṁ, Bhogānaṁ va pabhaṅguraṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

### 140 <a id="tea-dhammapada-140"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Or a fire burns his houses; and at the breaking up of the body the one of poor wisdom (duppañña) is reborn in hell (niraya).

> Atha vāssa agārāni, aggi ḍahati pāvako; Kāyassa bhedā duppañño, nirayaṁ sopapajjati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, world-fate_

terms: [duppañña](../terms/duppanna.md), [niraya](../terms/niraya.md)

### 141 <a id="tea-dhammapada-141"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Neither going naked (naggacariyā), nor matted hair, nor mud, nor not eating (anāsaka), nor lying on bare ground, nor dust and dirt, nor squatting exertion (ukkuṭikappadhāna) purifies a mortal who has not crossed over doubt (avitiṇṇakaṅkha).

> Bahubhaṇḍikabhikkhuvatthu Na naggacariyā na jaṭā na paṅkā, Nānāsakā thaṇḍilasāyikā vā; Rajojallaṁ ukkuṭikappadhānaṁ, Sodhenti maccaṁ avitiṇṇakaṅkhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: polemical · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [kaṅkhā](../terms/kankha.md)

### 142 <a id="tea-dhammapada-142"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even if adorned, if one conducts oneself evenly (samaṁ careyya) — calm (santa), tamed (danta), settled (niyata), leading the holy life (brahmacārī) — having laid down the rod towards all beings: he is a brāhmaṇa, he is a samaṇa, he is a bhikkhu.

> Santatimahāmattavatthu Alaṅkato cepi samaṁ careyya, Santo danto niyato brahmacārī; Sabbesu bhūtesu nidhāya daṇḍaṁ, So brāhmaṇo so samaṇo sa bhikkhu.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: realized · types: ethics_

terms: [brāhmaṇa](../terms/brahmana.md), [śramaṇa](../terms/sramana.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [daṇḍa](../terms/danda-pali.md) · concepts: [Śramaṇa and brāhmaṇa](../concepts/sramana-brahmana-contrast.md)

### 143 <a id="tea-dhammapada-143"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Is there anyone found in the world so restrained by shame (hirīnisedha) that he wakes up from sleep (niddā), as a fine horse does at the whip?

> Pilotikatissattheravatthu Hirīnisedho puriso, koci lokasmi vijjati; Yo niddaṁ apabodheti, asso bhadro kasāmiva.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [hiri](../terms/hiri.md), [niddā](../terms/nidda.md)

### 144 <a id="tea-dhammapada-144"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Like a fine horse touched by the whip, be ardent (ātāpī) and stirred (saṃvegī): with faith (saddhā), virtue (sīla), energy (vīriya), concentration (samādhi) and discernment of the Dhamma (dhammavinicchaya), endowed with knowledge and conduct (vijjācaraṇasampanna) and mindful (patissata), you will abandon this suffering (dukkha) that is not small.

> Asso yathā bhadro kasāniviṭṭho, Ātāpino saṁvegino bhavātha; Saddhāya sīlena ca vīriyena ca, Samādhinā dhammavinicchayena ca; Sampannavijjācaraṇā patissatā, Jahissatha dukkhamidaṁ anappakaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [ātāpī](../terms/atapi.md), [saṃvega](../terms/samvega-pali.md), [saddhā](../terms/saddha.md), [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [viriya](../terms/viriya.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md), [dhammavinicchaya](../terms/dhammavinicchaya.md), [vijjācaraṇa](../terms/vijjacarana.md), [sati](../terms/sati.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 145 <a id="tea-dhammapada-145"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Irrigators lead the water, fletchers bend the arrow, carpenters bend timber; those of good vows (subbatā) tame themselves (attānaṁ damayanti).

> Sukhasāmaṇeravatthu Udakañhi nayanti nettikā, Usukārā namayanti tejanaṁ; Dāruṁ namayanti tacchakā, Attānaṁ damayanti subbatā. Daṇḍavaggo dasamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md)

### 146 <a id="tea-dhammapada-146"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

What laughter, what delight, when there is constant burning (niccaṁ pajjalite)? Enveloped in darkness (andhakāra), will you not look for a lamp (padīpa)?

> Ko nu hāso kimānando, niccaṁ pajjalite sati; Andhakārena onaddhā, padīpaṁ na gavesatha.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

### 147 <a id="tea-dhammapada-147"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Look at this painted image (cittakataṁ bimbaṁ), a body of sores (arukāya), propped up, sick, full of many intentions (bahusaṅkappa), with no lasting stability (dhuvaṁ ṭhiti).

> Sirimāvatthu Passa cittakataṁ bimbaṁ, arukāyaṁ samussitaṁ; Āturaṁ bahusaṅkappaṁ, yassa natthi dhuvaṁ ṭhiti.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: body-layers_

### 148 <a id="tea-dhammapada-148"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This body (rūpa) is worn out, a nest of disease (roganīḷa), fragile; the foul mass (pūtisandeha) breaks up, for life ends in death.

> Uttarātherīvatthu Parijiṇṇamidaṁ rūpaṁ, roganīḷaṁ pabhaṅguraṁ; Bhijjati pūtisandeho, maraṇantañhi jīvitaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: body-layers, death-dying_

terms: [rūpa](../terms/rupa.md)

### 149 <a id="tea-dhammapada-149"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

These scattered bones, dove-coloured, like gourds thrown away in autumn: seeing them, what delight (rati)?

> Sambahulaadhimānikabhikkhuvatthu Yānimāni apatthāni, alābūneva sārade; Kāpotakāni aṭṭhīni, tāni disvāna kā rati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: body-layers_

### 150 <a id="tea-dhammapada-150"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

A city has been made of bones, plastered with flesh and blood, in which ageing (jarā) and death (maccu), conceit (māna) and hypocrisy (makkha) are stored.

> Janapadakalyāṇīrūpanandātherīvatthu Aṭṭhīnaṁ nagaraṁ kataṁ, maṁsalohitalepanaṁ; Yattha jarā ca maccu ca, māno makkho ca ohito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: body-layers, death-dying_

terms: [jarā](../terms/jara.md), [maccu](../terms/maccu.md), [māna](../terms/mana.md), [makkha](../terms/makkha.md) · obstacles: [Conceit (māna)](../obstacles/mana.md)

### 151 <a id="tea-dhammapada-151"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The splendid royal chariots wear out, and the body too comes to old age; but the Dhamma of the good (sataṁ dhammo) does not come to old age: so the good declare to one another.

> Mallikādevīvatthu Jīranti ve rājarathā sucittā, Atho sarīrampi jaraṁ upeti; Satañca dhammo na jaraṁ upeti, Santo have sabbhi pavedayanti.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [jarā](../terms/jara.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md)

### 152 <a id="tea-dhammapada-152"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This man of little learning (appassuta) grows old like an ox: his flesh grows, his wisdom (paññā) does not.

> Lāḷudāyītheravatthu Appassutāyaṁ puriso, balībaddhova jīrati; Maṁsāni tassa vaḍḍhanti, paññā tassa na vaḍḍhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [appassuta](../terms/appassuta.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [jarā](../terms/jara.md)

### 153 <a id="tea-dhammapada-153"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For many births I wandered in saṃsāra, not finding (anibbisaṁ) the house-builder (gahakāra) I was seeking: painful is birth again and again.

> Udānavatthu Anekajātisaṁsāraṁ, sandhāvissaṁ anibbisaṁ; Gahakāraṁ gavesanto, dukkhā jāti punappunaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [saṃsāra](../terms/samsara.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md), [jāti](../terms/jati.md) · concepts: [Saṃsāra](../concepts/samsara.md)

### 153-154 <a id="tea-dhammapada-153-154"></a>
`skeleton` · confidence high

Through many births I wandered in saṃsāra seeking the house-builder; painful is birth again and again. House-builder, you are seen! You will not build a house again; all your rafters are broken, the ridge-pole shattered; the mind has gone to the unconditioned, it has reached the destruction of cravings.

> Anekajātisaṁsāraṁ, sandhāvissaṁ anibbisaṁ; Gahakāraṁ gavesanto, dukkhā jāti punappunaṁ. Gahakāraka diṭṭhosi, puna gehaṁ na kāhasi; Sabbā te phāsukā bhaggā, gahakūṭaṁ visaṅkhataṁ; Visaṅkhāragataṁ cittaṁ, taṇhānaṁ khayamajjhagā.

_level: ultimate · standpoint: experiential · path: knowledge · stage: realized · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [taṇhakkhaya](../terms/tanhakkhaya.md), [visaṅkhāra](../terms/visankhara.md) · concepts: [The awakening of the Buddha](../concepts/awakening-of-the-buddha.md) · teachers: [Gotama Buddha](../teachers/gotama-buddha.md)

_Superseded by [153](dhammapada.md#tea-dhammapada-153)_

### 154 <a id="tea-dhammapada-154"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

House-builder, you are seen! You will not build a house again. All your rafters are broken, the ridge-pole is demolished; the mind, gone to the unformed (visaṅkhāragata), has reached the destruction of cravings (taṇhānaṁ khaya).

> Gahakāraka diṭṭhosi, puna gehaṁ na kāhasi; Sabbā te phāsukā bhaggā, gahakūṭaṁ visaṅkhataṁ; Visaṅkhāragataṁ cittaṁ, taṇhānaṁ khayamajjhagā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: realized · types: karma-liberation, ultimate_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [visaṅkhāra](../terms/visankhara.md), [taṇhakkhaya](../terms/tanhakkhaya.md)

### 155 <a id="tea-dhammapada-155"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not having lived the holy life (brahmacariya), not having gained wealth in youth, they brood like old herons in a pond without fish.

> Mahādhanaseṭṭhiputtavatthu Acaritvā brahmacariyaṁ, aladdhā yobbane dhanaṁ; Jiṇṇakoñcāva jhāyanti, khīṇamaccheva pallale.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, death-dying_

terms: [brahmacariya](../terms/brahmacariya.md)

### 156 <a id="tea-dhammapada-156"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Not having lived the holy life, not having gained wealth in youth, they lie like spent arrows (cāpātikhīṇāva), lamenting over the past.

> Acaritvā brahmacariyaṁ, aladdhā yobbane dhanaṁ; Senti cāpātikhīṇāva, purāṇāni anutthunaṁ. Jarāvaggo ekādasamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, death-dying_

terms: [brahmacariya](../terms/brahmacariya.md)

### 157 <a id="tea-dhammapada-157"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

If one holds oneself dear, one should guard oneself well; the wise one should keep watch over oneself during one of the three watches (yāma).

> Attānañce piyaṁ jaññā, rakkheyya naṁ surakkhitaṁ; Tiṇṇaṁ aññataraṁ yāmaṁ, paṭijaggeyya paṇḍito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md), [yāma](../terms/yama-watch.md)

### 158 <a id="tea-dhammapada-158"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should first establish oneself in what is suitable (patirūpa); only then should one instruct others: thus the wise one is not defiled.

> Upanandasakyaputtattheravatthu Attānameva paṭhamaṁ, patirūpe nivesaye; Athaññamanusāseyya, na kilisseyya paṇḍito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md)

### 159 <a id="tea-dhammapada-159"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should do oneself as one instructs another: well tamed, one may then tame (others); for the self (attā) is indeed hard to tame.

> Padhānikatissattheravatthu Attānañce tathā kayirā, yathāññamanusāsati; Sudanto vata dametha, attā hi kira duddamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md)

### 160 <a id="tea-dhammapada-160"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Oneself is one's own protector (nātha); what other protector could there be? With oneself well tamed one gains a protector hard to find.

> Kumārakassapamātuttherivatthu Attā hi attano nātho, ko hi nātho paro siyā; Attanā hi sudantena, nāthaṁ labhati dullabhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md), [nātha](../terms/natha-pali.md) · concepts: [Oneself as island, the Dhamma as island](../concepts/attadipa.md)

### 161 <a id="tea-dhammapada-161"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Evil done by oneself, born of oneself, produced from oneself, grinds down the fool as a diamond grinds a gem of stone.

> Mahākālaupāsakavatthu Attanā hi kataṁ pāpaṁ, Attajaṁ attasambhavaṁ; Abhimatthati dummedhaṁ, Vajiraṁvasmamayaṁ maṇiṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [attā](../terms/atta.md)

### 162 <a id="tea-dhammapada-162"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One whose bad conduct is extreme, like a māluvā creeper spread over a sāla tree, does to himself what his enemy would wish.

> Devadattavatthu Yassa accantadussilyaṁ, māluvā sālamivotthataṁ; Karoti so tathattānaṁ, yathā naṁ icchatī diso.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [dussīlya](../terms/dussilya.md)

### 163 <a id="tea-dhammapada-163"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Bad things, harmful to oneself, are easy to do; but what is beneficial and good is very hard to do.

> Saṅghabhedaparisakkanavatthu Sukarāni asādhūni, attano ahitāni ca; Yaṁ ve hitañca sādhuñca, taṁ ve paramadukkaraṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

### 164 <a id="tea-dhammapada-164"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The fool who reviles the teaching of the arahants, the noble ones who live by the Dhamma, relying on a wicked view (diṭṭhi pāpikā), bears fruit for his own destruction, like the fruits of the kaṭṭhaka reed.

> Kālattheravatthu Yo sāsanaṁ arahataṁ, ariyānaṁ dhammajīvinaṁ; Paṭikkosati dummedho, diṭṭhiṁ nissāya pāpikaṁ; Phalāni kaṭṭhakasseva, attaghātāya phallati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [arahant](../terms/arahant.md), [diṭṭhi](../terms/ditthi.md)

### 165 <a id="tea-dhammapada-165"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

By oneself is evil done, by oneself one is defiled; by oneself evil is left undone, by oneself one is purified. Purity and impurity are one's own (paccatta): no one can purify another.

> Cūḷakālaupāsakavatthu Attanā hi kataṁ pāpaṁ, attanā saṅkilissati; Attanā akataṁ pāpaṁ, attanāva visujjhati; Suddhī asuddhi paccattaṁ, nāñño aññaṁ visodhaye.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general, action · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [attā](../terms/atta.md) · concepts: [Kamma](../concepts/kamma.md)

### 166 <a id="tea-dhammapada-166"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One should not neglect one's own welfare (attadattha) for another's, however great; knowing one's own welfare, one should be devoted to one's own good (sadattha).

> Attadatthattheravatthu Attadatthaṁ paratthena, bahunāpi na hāpaye; Attadatthamabhiññāya, sadatthapasuto siyā. Attavaggo dvādasamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [attadattha](../terms/attadattha.md)

### 167 <a id="tea-dhammapada-167"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One should not follow the base (hīna) dhamma, nor live in heedlessness (pamāda), nor follow wrong view (micchādiṭṭhi); one should not be an increaser of the world (lokavaḍḍhana).

> Hīnaṁ dhammaṁ na seveyya, pamādena na saṁvase; Micchādiṭṭhiṁ na seveyya, na siyā lokavaḍḍhano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [micchādiṭṭhi](../terms/micchaditthi.md), [lokavaḍḍhana](../terms/lokavaddhana.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 168 <a id="tea-dhammapada-168"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should rise up and not be heedless; one should practise the Dhamma well; one who practises the Dhamma lives happily in this world and the next.

> Suddhodanavatthu Uttiṭṭhe nappamajjeyya, dhammaṁ sucaritaṁ care; Dhammacārī sukhaṁ seti, asmiṁ loke paramhi ca.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md)

### 169 <a id="tea-dhammapada-169"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should practise the Dhamma well and not practise wrong conduct (duccarita); one who practises the Dhamma lives happily in this world and the next.

> Dhammaṁ care sucaritaṁ, na naṁ duccaritaṁ care; Dhammacārī sukhaṁ seti, asmiṁ loke paramhi ca.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 170 <a id="tea-dhammapada-170"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should look at the world as one looks at a bubble, as one looks at a mirage: the King of Death does not see one who regards the world in this way.

> Pañcasatavipassakabhikkhuvatthu Yathā pubbuḷakaṁ passe, yathā passe marīcikaṁ; Evaṁ lokaṁ avekkhantaṁ, maccurājā na passati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: knowledge · stage: advanced · types: practice, death-dying_

terms: [maccurāja](../terms/maccuraja.md)

### 171 <a id="tea-dhammapada-171"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Come, look at this world, adorned (cittaṁ) like a royal chariot: fools sink in it, but there is no attachment (saṅga) for those who know.

> Abhayarājakumāravatthu Etha passathimaṁ lokaṁ, cittaṁ rājarathūpamaṁ; Yattha bālā visīdanti, natthi saṅgo vijānataṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: knowledge · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [saṅga](../terms/sanga-pali.md)

### 172 <a id="tea-dhammapada-172"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who was heedless before and afterwards is not heedless, lights up this world like the moon freed from a cloud.

> Sammajjanattheravatthu Yo ca pubbe pamajjitvā, pacchā so nappamajjati; Somaṁ lokaṁ pabhāseti, abbhā muttova candimā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md)

### 173 <a id="tea-dhammapada-173"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One whose evil deed (pāpaṁ kammaṁ) done is covered by the wholesome (kusala), lights up this world like the moon freed from a cloud.

> Aṅgulimālattheravatthu Yassa pāpaṁ kataṁ kammaṁ, kusalena pidhīyati; Somaṁ lokaṁ pabhāseti, abbhā muttova candimā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [kusala](../terms/kusala.md), [kamma](../terms/kamma.md)

### 174 <a id="tea-dhammapada-174"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This world is blind; few here see clearly. Like a bird freed from a net, few go to heaven (sagga).

> Pesakāradhītāvatthu Andhabhūto ayaṁ loko, tanukettha vipassati; Sakuṇo jālamuttova, appo saggāya gacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: world-fate_

terms: [sagga](../terms/sagga.md)

### 175 <a id="tea-dhammapada-175"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Swans travel the sun's path; they travel through the sky by psychic power (iddhi); the steadfast (dhīra) are led out of the world, having conquered Māra with his army.

> Tiṁsabhikkhuvatthu Haṁsādiccapathe yanti, ākāse yanti iddhiyā; Nīyanti dhīrā lokamhā, jetvā māraṁ savāhiniṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: realized · types: powers-experiences, world-fate_

### 176 <a id="tea-dhammapada-176"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For a person who has transgressed one dhamma, a liar (musāvādī) who has disregarded the world beyond (paraloka), there is no evil he would not do.

> Ciñcamāṇavikāvatthu Ekaṁ dhammaṁ atītassa, musāvādissa jantuno; Vitiṇṇaparalokassa, natthi pāpaṁ akāriyaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

### 177 <a id="tea-dhammapada-177"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Misers (kadariya) do not go to the world of the gods; fools do not praise giving (dāna); but the steadfast one who rejoices in giving is thereby happy in the next world.

> Asadisadānavatthu Na ve kadariyā devalokaṁ vajanti, Bālā have nappasaṁsanti dānaṁ; Dhīro ca dānaṁ anumodamāno, Teneva so hoti sukhī parattha.

### 178 <a id="tea-dhammapada-178"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better than sole sovereignty over the earth, than going to heaven, than lordship over all the worlds, is the fruit of stream-entry (sotāpattiphala).

> Anāthapiṇḍikaputtakālavatthu Pathabyā ekarajjena, saggassa gamanena vā; Sabbalokādhipaccena, sotāpattiphalaṁ varaṁ. Lokavaggo terasamo.

### 179 <a id="tea-dhammapada-179"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

The Buddha of endless range (anantagocara), whose victory is not undone and whose conquest no one in the world follows: by what track will you lead him, the trackless (apada)?

> Yassa jitaṁ nāvajīyati, Jitaṁ yassa noyāti koci loke; Taṁ buddhamanantagocaraṁ, Apadaṁ kena padena nessatha.

_level: conventional · standpoint: devotional · path: general · stage: realized · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [buddha](../terms/buddha.md)

### 180 <a id="tea-dhammapada-180"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

He in whom no entangling, clinging craving (taṇhā jālinī visattikā) exists anywhere to lead him: by what track will you lead that Buddha of endless range, the trackless?

> Yassa jālinī visattikā, Taṇhā natthi kuhiñci netave; Taṁ buddhamanantagocaraṁ, Apadaṁ kena padena nessatha.

_level: conventional · standpoint: devotional · path: general · stage: realized · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

`text-verified` · confidence high

The steadfast ones devoted to jhāna (jhānapasuta), delighting in renunciation (nekkhamma) and calm (upasama) — even the gods envy those fully awakened, mindful ones.

> Devorohaṇavatthu Ye jhānapasutā dhīrā, nekkhammūpasame ratā; Devāpi tesaṁ pihayanti, sambuddhānaṁ satīmataṁ.

### 182 <a id="tea-dhammapada-182"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Hard to gain is a human birth; hard is the life of mortals; hard is the hearing of the true Dhamma (saddhamma); hard is the arising of Buddhas.

> Erakapattanāgarājavatthu Kiccho manussapaṭilābho, kicchaṁ maccāna jīvitaṁ; Kicchaṁ saddhammassavanaṁ, kiccho buddhānamuppādo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: world-fate_

### 183 <a id="tea-dhammapada-183"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not doing any evil, undertaking the wholesome (kusala), purifying one's own mind: this is the teaching of the Buddhas (buddhāna sāsana).

> Ānandattherapañhavatthu Sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṁ, kusalassa upasampadā; Sacittapariyodapanaṁ, etaṁ buddhāna sāsanaṁ.

### 184 <a id="tea-dhammapada-184"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Patient endurance (khanti) is the highest austerity (tapa), forbearance (titikkhā); the Buddhas say nibbāna is supreme; one gone forth (pabbajita) who harms others is not one, and one who oppresses another is not a samaṇa.

> Khantī paramaṁ tapo titikkhā, Nibbānaṁ paramaṁ vadanti buddhā; Na hi pabbajito parūpaghātī, Na samaṇo hoti paraṁ viheṭhayanto.

`text-verified` · confidence high

Not reviling, not harming, restraint in the Pātimokkha, moderation in food, remote lodging, and application to the higher mind (adhicitta): this is the teaching of the Buddhas.

> Anūpavādo anūpaghāto, Pātimokkhe ca saṁvaro; Mattaññutā ca bhattasmiṁ, Pantañca sayanāsanaṁ; Adhicitte ca āyogo, Etaṁ buddhāna sāsanaṁ.

### 186 <a id="tea-dhammapada-186"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not even by a shower of coins is there satiety in sense pleasures (kāma); sense pleasures give little enjoyment and are painful: the wise one, knowing this,

> Anabhiratabhikkhuvatthu Na kahāpaṇavassena, titti kāmesu vijjati; Appassādā dukhā kāmā, iti viññāya paṇḍito.

### 187 <a id="tea-dhammapada-187"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

finds no delight even in heavenly pleasures (dibbā kāmā); the disciple of the perfectly awakened one (sammāsambuddhasāvaka) delights in the ending of craving (taṇhakkhaya).

> Api dibbesu kāmesu, ratiṁ so nādhigacchati; Taṇhakkhayarato hoti, sammāsambuddhasāvako.

### 188 <a id="tea-dhammapada-188"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Many people go for refuge (saraṇa), driven by fear, to mountains, forests, groves, trees and shrines.

> Aggidattabrāhmaṇavatthu Bahuṁ ve saraṇaṁ yanti, pabbatāni vanāni ca; Ārāmarukkhacetyāni, manussā bhayatajjitā.

### 189 <a id="tea-dhammapada-189"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

That is not a safe (khema) refuge, not the supreme refuge; having come to that refuge one is not freed from all suffering (dukkha).

> Netaṁ kho saraṇaṁ khemaṁ, netaṁ saraṇamuttamaṁ; Netaṁ saraṇamāgamma, sabbadukkhā pamuccati.

### 190 <a id="tea-dhammapada-190"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who has gone for refuge to the Buddha, the Dhamma and the Saṅgha sees with right wisdom the four noble truths:

> Yo ca buddhañca dhammañca, saṅghañca saraṇaṁ gato; Cattāri ariyasaccāni, sammappaññāya passati.

### 191 <a id="tea-dhammapada-191"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Suffering (dukkha), the origin of suffering, the overcoming of suffering, and the noble eightfold path (ariya aṭṭhaṅgika magga) leading to the calming of suffering.

> Dukkhaṁ dukkhasamuppādaṁ, dukkhassa ca atikkamaṁ; Ariyaṁ caṭṭhaṅgikaṁ maggaṁ, dukkhūpasamagāminaṁ.

### 192 <a id="tea-dhammapada-192"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

That is the safe refuge, that is the supreme refuge; having come to that refuge one is freed from all suffering.

> Etaṁ kho saraṇaṁ khemaṁ, etaṁ saraṇamuttamaṁ; Etaṁ saraṇamāgamma, sabbadukkhā pamuccati.

### 193 <a id="tea-dhammapada-193"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

A thoroughbred person (purisājañña) is hard to find; he is not born everywhere; the family where that steadfast one is born prospers happily.

> Ānandattherapañhavatthu Dullabho purisājañño, na so sabbattha jāyati; Yattha so jāyati dhīro, taṁ kulaṁ sukhamedhati.

### 194 <a id="tea-dhammapada-194"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Happy is the arising of Buddhas; happy is the teaching of the true Dhamma; happy is the harmony of the Saṅgha (saṅghassa sāmaggī); happy is the austerity (tapa) of those in harmony.

> Sambahulabhikkhuvatthu Sukho buddhānamuppādo, sukhā saddhammadesanā; Sukhā saṅghassa sāmaggī, samaggānaṁ tapo sukho.

### 195 <a id="tea-dhammapada-195"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who honours those worthy of honour, whether Buddhas or disciples, who have gone beyond proliferation (papañca) and crossed over sorrow and lamentation,

> Kassapadasabalassasuvaṇṇacetiyavatthu Pūjārahe pūjayato, buddhe yadi va sāvake; Papañcasamatikkante, tiṇṇasokapariddave.

_level: conventional · standpoint: devotional · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [buddha](../terms/buddha.md), [sāvaka](../terms/savaka.md), [papañca](../terms/papanca.md)

### 196 <a id="tea-dhammapada-196"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

— one who honours such ones, extinguished (nibbuta) and fearing nothing from any quarter: his merit (puñña) cannot be measured by anyone as 'so much'.

> Te tādise pūjayato, nibbute akutobhaye; Na sakkā puññaṁ saṅkhātuṁ, imettamapi kenaci. Buddhavaggo cuddasamo. Paṭhamabhāṇavāraṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: devotional · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

### 197 <a id="tea-dhammapada-197"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Happily indeed we live, without hatred (averino) among the hateful (verinesu); among hateful people we dwell without hatred.

> Susukhaṁ vata jīvāma, verinesu averino; Verinesu manussesu, viharāma averino.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general, action · stage: all · types: ethics_

### 198 <a id="tea-dhammapada-198"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Happily indeed we live, free of affliction (anāturā) among the afflicted (āturesu); among afflicted people we dwell free of affliction.

> Susukhaṁ vata jīvāma, āturesu anāturā; Āturesu manussesu, viharāma anāturā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 199 <a id="tea-dhammapada-199"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Happily indeed we live, without eagerness (anussukā) among the eager (ussukesu); among eager people we dwell without eagerness.

> Susukhaṁ vata jīvāma, ussukesu anussukā; Ussukesu manussesu, viharāma anussukā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 200 <a id="tea-dhammapada-200"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Happily indeed we live, we who have nothing of our own (kiñcana): we shall feed on joy (pīti), like the radiant gods (ābhassarā devā).

> Māravatthu Susukhaṁ vata jīvāma, yesaṁ no natthi kiñcanaṁ; Pītibhakkhā bhavissāma, devā ābhassarā yathā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [akiñcana](../terms/akincana.md), [pīti](../terms/piti.md), [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md)

### 201 <a id="tea-dhammapada-201"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Victory breeds hatred (vera); the defeated lies down in suffering (dukkha); the calmed one (upasanta) lies down happily (sukha), having given up victory and defeat.

> Kosalaraññoparājayavatthu Jayaṁ veraṁ pasavati, dukkhaṁ seti parājito; Upasanto sukhaṁ seti, hitvā jayaparājayaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [vera](../terms/vera.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md), [upasama](../terms/upasama.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 202 <a id="tea-dhammapada-202"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

There is no fire like passion (rāga), no ill-luck like hatred (dosa), no suffering like the aggregates (khandha), no happiness higher than peace (santi).

> Aññatarakuladārikāvatthu Natthi rāgasamo aggi, Natthi dosasamo kali; Natthi khandhasamā dukkhā, Natthi santiparaṁ sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

### 203 <a id="tea-dhammapada-203"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Hunger is the worst of afflictions (jighacchā paramā rogā), formations (saṅkhāra) the worst of sufferings; knowing this as it really is (yathābhūtaṁ), nibbāna is the supreme happiness.

> Ekaupāsakavatthu Jighacchāparamā rogā, saṅkhāraparamā dukhā; Etaṁ ñatvā yathābhūtaṁ, nibbānaṁ paramaṁ sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, ultimate_

### 203-204 <a id="tea-dhammapada-203-204"></a>
`skeleton` · confidence high

Hunger is the greatest illness, formations the greatest suffering; knowing this as it is, nibbāna is the highest happiness. Health is the highest gain, contentment the greatest wealth, trust the best kinsman, nibbāna the highest happiness.

> Jighacchāparamā rogā, saṅkhāraparamā dukhā; Etaṁ ñatvā yathābhūtaṁ, nibbānaṁ paramaṁ sukhaṁ. Ārogyaparamā lābhā, Santuṭṭhiparamaṁ dhanaṁ; Vissāsaparamā ñāti, Nibbānaṁ paramaṁ sukhaṁ.

_level: ultimate · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md) · concepts: [Nibbāna](../concepts/nibbana.md)

### 204 <a id="tea-dhammapada-204"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Health (ārogya) is the highest gain, contentment (santuṭṭhi) the highest wealth, trust (vissāsa) the highest kinship; nibbāna is the supreme happiness.

> Pasenadikosalavatthu Ārogyaparamā lābhā, Santuṭṭhiparamaṁ dhanaṁ; Vissāsaparamā ñāti, Nibbānaṁ paramaṁ sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ultimate, ethics_

terms: [ārogya](../terms/arogya.md), [santuṭṭhi](../terms/santutthi.md), [vissāsa](../terms/vissasa.md), [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md) · concepts: [Nibbāna](../concepts/nibbana.md)

### 205 <a id="tea-dhammapada-205"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Having drunk the taste of seclusion (paviveka) and the taste of calm (upasama), one becomes free of distress (niddara) and free of evil (nippāpa), drinking the taste of joy in the Dhamma (dhammapīti).

> Tissattheravatthu Pavivekarasaṁ pitvā, rasaṁ upasamassa ca; Niddaro hoti nippāpo, dhammapītirasaṁ pivaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [viveka](../terms/viveka.md), [upasama](../terms/upasama.md), [dhammapīti](../terms/dhammapiti.md) · practices: [Seclusion (viveka)](../practices/viveka.md)

### 206 <a id="tea-dhammapada-206"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Good is the sight of the noble ones (ariya); living together with them is always happy; by not seeing fools (bāla) one would always be happy.

> Sakkavatthu Sāhu dassanamariyānaṁ, sannivāso sadā sukho; Adassanena bālānaṁ, niccameva sukhī siyā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [ariya](../terms/ariya.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 207 <a id="tea-dhammapada-207"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who goes about in the company of fools grieves for a long time; living with fools is painful, like always being with an enemy; living with the steadfast (dhīra) is pleasant, like the meeting of kin.

> Bālasaṅgatacārī hi, dīghamaddhāna socati; Dukkho bālehi saṁvāso, amitteneva sabbadā; Dhīro ca sukhasaṁvāso, ñātīnaṁva samāgamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [dhīra](../terms/dhira-pali.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 208 <a id="tea-dhammapada-208"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Therefore, one should keep company with the steadfast, the wise, the learned (bahussuta), the noble one (ariya) of yoke-bearing virtue (dhorayhasīla) and vows: such a good person (sappurisa) of good wisdom, as the moon follows the path of the stars.

> Tasmā hi— Dhīrañca paññañca bahussutañca, Dhorayhasīlaṁ vatavantamariyaṁ; Taṁ tādisaṁ sappurisaṁ sumedhaṁ, Bhajetha nakkhattapathaṁva candimā. Sukhavaggo pannarasamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [dhīra](../terms/dhira-pali.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [bahussuta](../terms/bahussuta.md), [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [sappurisa](../terms/sappurisa.md), [ariya](../terms/ariya.md)

### 209 <a id="tea-dhammapada-209"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Applying oneself to what is not application (ayoga) and not applying oneself to application (yoga), giving up the goal (attha) and grasping at what is dear (piyaggāhī), one comes to envy those who devote themselves to their own training (attānuyoginaṁ).

> Ayoge yuñjamattānaṁ, yogasmiñca ayojayaṁ; Atthaṁ hitvā piyaggāhī, pihetattānuyoginaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

terms: [yoga](../terms/yoga-pali.md), [piya](../terms/piya.md), [attha](../terms/attha.md)

### 210 <a id="tea-dhammapada-210"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Do not come together with the dear (piya), nor ever with the undear (appiya): not seeing the dear is suffering, and seeing the undear is (suffering).

> Mā piyehi samāgañchi, appiyehi kudācanaṁ; Piyānaṁ adassanaṁ dukkhaṁ, appiyānañca dassanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [piya](../terms/piya.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 211 <a id="tea-dhammapada-211"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Therefore do not make anything dear (piya), for separation from the dear is bad; those for whom there is neither dear nor undear have no ties (gantha).

> Tasmā piyaṁ na kayirātha, piyāpāyo hi pāpako; Ganthā tesaṁ na vijjanti, yesaṁ natthi piyāppiyaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, ethics_

terms: [piya](../terms/piya.md), [gantha](../terms/gantha.md)

### 212 <a id="tea-dhammapada-212"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

From the dear (piya) grief (soka) is born, from the dear fear (bhaya) is born; for one wholly freed from the dear there is no grief: whence fear?

> Aññatarakuṭumbikavatthu Piyato jāyatī soko, piyato jāyatī bhayaṁ; Piyato vippamuttassa, natthi soko kuto bhayaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, ethics_

terms: [piya](../terms/piya.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md), [bhaya](../terms/bhaya.md) · obstacles: [Grief (soka)](../obstacles/soka-pali.md)

### 213 <a id="tea-dhammapada-213"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

From affection (pema) grief is born, from affection fear is born; for one wholly freed from affection there is no grief: whence fear?

> Visākhāvatthu Pemato jāyatī soko, pemato jāyatī bhayaṁ; Pemato vippamuttassa, natthi soko kuto bhayaṁ.

From delight (rati) grief is born, from delight fear is born; for one wholly freed from delight there is no grief: whence fear?

> Licchavīvatthu Ratiyā jāyatī soko, ratiyā jāyatī bhayaṁ; Ratiyā vippamuttassa, natthi soko kuto bhayaṁ.

From sensual desire (kāma) grief is born, from sensual desire fear is born; for one wholly freed from sensual desire there is no grief: whence fear?

> Anitthigandhakumāravatthu Kāmato jāyatī soko, kāmato jāyatī bhayaṁ; Kāmato vippamuttassa, natthi soko kuto bhayaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, ethics_

### 216 <a id="tea-dhammapada-216"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

From craving (taṇhā) grief is born, from craving fear is born; for one wholly freed from craving there is no grief: whence fear?

> Aññatarabrāhmaṇavatthu Taṇhāya jāyatī soko, taṇhāya jāyatī bhayaṁ; Taṇhāya vippamuttassa, natthi soko kuto bhayaṁ.

### 217 <a id="tea-dhammapada-217"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One endowed with virtue and vision (sīladassanasampanna), standing in the Dhamma (dhammaṭṭha), who knows the truth (saccavedī) and does his own work: people hold him dear.

> Pañcasatadārakavatthu Sīladassanasampannaṁ, dhammaṭṭhaṁ saccavedinaṁ; Attano kamma kubbānaṁ, taṁ jano kurute piyaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [sacca](../terms/sacca.md)

### 218 <a id="tea-dhammapada-218"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

One in whom desire (chanda) has arisen for the undeclared (anakkhāta), who would be suffused by mind (manasā phuṭo), and whose mind is not bound to sense pleasures, is called 'one going upstream' (uddhaṁsota).

> Ekaanāgāmittheravatthu Chandajāto anakkhāte, Manasā ca phuṭo siyā; Kāmesu ca appaṭibaddhacitto, Uddhaṁsototi vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: advanced · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [chanda](../terms/chanda.md), [anakkhāta](../terms/anakkhata.md), [kāma](../terms/kama.md), [uddhaṃsota](../terms/uddhamsota.md)

### 219 <a id="tea-dhammapada-219"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

A man long away, returned safely from afar, is welcomed on his return by relatives, friends and well-wishers.

> Nandiyavatthu Cirappavāsiṁ purisaṁ, dūrato sotthimāgataṁ; Ñātimittā suhajjā ca, abhinandanti āgataṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

### 220 <a id="tea-dhammapada-220"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

In the same way, one who has made merit (katapuñña) and has gone from this world to the next: his merits welcome him, as kin welcome a dear one who has returned.

> Tatheva katapuññampi, asmā lokā paraṁ gataṁ; Puññāni paṭigaṇhanti, piyaṁ ñātīva āgataṁ. Piyavaggo soḷasamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying, karma-liberation_

terms: [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md)

### 221 <a id="tea-dhammapada-221"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should abandon anger (kodha), give up conceit (māna), and overcome every fetter (saṃyojana); sufferings do not fall upon one who does not cling to name-and-form (nāmarūpa) and has nothing (akiñcana).

> Kodhaṁ jahe vippajaheyya mānaṁ, Saṁyojanaṁ sabbamatikkameyya; Taṁ nāmarūpasmimasajjamānaṁ, Akiñcanaṁ nānupatanti dukkhā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [kodha](../terms/kodha.md), [māna](../terms/mana.md), [saṁyojana](../terms/samyojana.md), [nāmarūpa](../terms/namarupa.md), [akiñcana](../terms/akincana.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md) · obstacles: [Anger (kodha)](../obstacles/kodha.md), [Conceit (māna)](../obstacles/mana.md)

### 222 <a id="tea-dhammapada-222"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whoever restrains arisen anger like a swerving chariot, that one I call a charioteer (sārathi); other people merely hold the reins.

> Aññatarabhikkhuvatthu Yo ve uppatitaṁ kodhaṁ, rathaṁ bhantaṁva vāraye; Tamahaṁ sārathiṁ brūmi, rasmiggāho itaro jano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [kodha](../terms/kodha.md), [sārathi](../terms/sarathi-pali.md) · obstacles: [Anger (kodha)](../obstacles/kodha.md)

### 223 <a id="tea-dhammapada-223"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should conquer anger by non-anger (akkodha), the bad (asādhu) by the good (sādhu), the stingy (kadariya) by giving (dāna), the speaker of falsehood by truth (sacca).

> Uttarāupāsikavatthu Akkodhena jine kodhaṁ, asādhuṁ sādhunā jine; Jine kadariyaṁ dānena, saccenālikavādinaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general, action · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [kodha](../terms/kodha.md), [akkodha](../terms/akkodha.md), [kadariya](../terms/kadariya.md), [dāna](../terms/dana.md), [sacca](../terms/sacca.md) · practices: [Giving (dāna)](../practices/dana.md) · obstacles: [Anger (kodha)](../obstacles/kodha.md)

### 224 <a id="tea-dhammapada-224"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Speak the truth, do not become angry, give even a little when asked: by these three things one may go into the presence of the gods.

> Mahāmoggallānapañhavatthu Saccaṁ bhaṇe na kujjheyya, dajjā appampi yācito; Etehi tīhi ṭhānehi, gacche devāna santike.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sacca](../terms/sacca.md), [kodha](../terms/kodha.md), [dāna](../terms/dana.md), [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md) · practices: [Giving (dāna)](../practices/dana.md) · obstacles: [Anger (kodha)](../obstacles/kodha.md)

### 225 <a id="tea-dhammapada-225"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

The sages (muni) who are non-harming (ahiṁsaka), ever restrained in body, go to the unshakeable place (accutaṁ ṭhānaṁ), where, having gone, they do not grieve.

> Buddhapitubrāhmaṇavatthu Ahiṁsakā ye munayo, niccaṁ kāyena saṁvutā; Te yanti accutaṁ ṭhānaṁ, yattha gantvā na socare.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: realized · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [muni](../terms/muni.md), [ahiṃsā](../terms/ahimsa-pali.md), [accutaṭṭhāna](../terms/accutatthana.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md) · practices: [Non-harming (ahiṃsā)](../practices/ahimsa-pali.md)

### 226 <a id="tea-dhammapada-226"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For those who are always wide awake (jāgaramāna), who train day and night, and who are intent on nibbāna, the taints (āsavā) come to an end.

> Puṇṇadāsīvatthu Sadā jāgaramānānaṁ, ahorattānusikkhinaṁ; Nibbānaṁ adhimuttānaṁ, atthaṁ gacchanti āsavā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: advanced · types: practice, karma-liberation_

### 227 <a id="tea-dhammapada-227"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This is ancient, Atula, not just of today: they blame one who sits silent, they blame one who speaks much, they blame even one who speaks moderately; there is no one in the world who is not blamed.

> Atulaupāsakavatthu Porāṇametaṁ atula, netaṁ ajjatanāmiva; Nindanti tuṇhimāsīnaṁ, nindanti bahubhāṇinaṁ; Mitabhāṇimpi nindanti, natthi loke anindito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: world-fate_

terms: [nindā](../terms/ninda-pali.md)

### 228 <a id="tea-dhammapada-228"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

There never was, there never will be, nor is there now, anyone who is wholly blamed or wholly praised.

> Na cāhu na ca bhavissati, na cetarahi vijjati; Ekantaṁ nindito poso, ekantaṁ vā pasaṁsito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: world-fate_

terms: [nindā](../terms/ninda-pali.md), [pasaṃsā](../terms/pasamsa.md)

### 229 <a id="tea-dhammapada-229"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One whom the discerning praise day after day, having considered him: of faultless conduct, wise, composed in wisdom and virtue,

> Yañce viññū pasaṁsanti, anuvicca suve suve; Acchiddavuttiṁ medhāviṁ, paññāsīlasamāhitaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [viññū](../terms/vinnu.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md)

### 230 <a id="tea-dhammapada-230"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

— like a coin of Jambonada gold, who is fit to blame him? Even the gods praise him; he is praised even by Brahmā.

> Nikkhaṁ jambonadasseva, ko taṁ ninditumarahati; Devāpi naṁ pasaṁsanti, brahmunāpi pasaṁsito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md)

### 231 <a id="tea-dhammapada-231"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should guard against agitation of the body (kāyappakopa) and be restrained in body; having abandoned bad conduct of body, one should practise good conduct by body.

> Chabbaggiyavatthu Kāyappakopaṁ rakkheyya, kāyena saṁvuto siyā; Kāyaduccaritaṁ hitvā, kāyena sucaritaṁ care.

### 232 <a id="tea-dhammapada-232"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should guard against agitation of speech (vacīpakopa) and be restrained in speech; having abandoned bad conduct of speech, one should practise good conduct by speech.

> Vacīpakopaṁ rakkheyya, vācāya saṁvuto siyā; Vacīduccaritaṁ hitvā, vācāya sucaritaṁ care.

`text-verified` · confidence high

> Manopakopaṁ rakkheyya, manasā saṁvuto siyā; Manoduccaritaṁ hitvā, manasā sucaritaṁ care.
