### 234 <a id="tea-dhammapada-234"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The steadfast (dhīra) who are restrained in body, then in speech, and restrained in mind: they are indeed well restrained.

> Kāyena saṁvutā dhīrā, atho vācāya saṁvutā; Manasā saṁvutā dhīrā, te ve suparisaṁvutā. Kodhavaggo sattarasamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: advanced · types: ethics_

terms: [dhīra](../terms/dhira-pali.md), [saṃvara](../terms/samvara-pali.md)

### 235 <a id="tea-dhammapada-235"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Now you are like a withered leaf; Yama's men have come near you; you stand at the mouth of departure (uyyogamukha), and you have no provisions (pātheyya) for the way.

> Paṇḍupalāsova dānisi, Yamapurisāpi ca te upaṭṭhitā; Uyyogamukhe ca tiṭṭhasi, Pātheyyampi ca te na vijjati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [yama](../terms/yama-pali.md)

### 236 <a id="tea-dhammapada-236"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

So make an island (dīpa) for yourself; strive quickly, be wise; with your stains blown away (niddhantamala) and blameless (anaṅgaṇa), you will reach the divine noble ground (dibbaṁ ariyabhūmiṁ).

> So karohi dīpamattano, Khippaṁ vāyama paṇḍito bhava; Niddhantamalo anaṅgaṇo, Dibbaṁ ariyabhūmiṁ upehisi.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [dīpa](../terms/dipa.md), [mala](../terms/mala-pali.md) · concepts: [Oneself as island, the Dhamma as island](../concepts/attadipa.md)

### 237 <a id="tea-dhammapada-237"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Your age has run down (upanītavaya); you have set out to the presence of Yama; there is no resting place for you on the way, and you have no provisions.

> Upanītavayo ca dānisi, Sampayātosi yamassa santikaṁ; Vāso te natthi antarā, Pātheyyampi ca te na vijjati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [yama](../terms/yama-pali.md)

### 238 <a id="tea-dhammapada-238"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

So make an island for yourself; strive quickly, be wise; with your stains blown away and blameless, you will not again come to birth and ageing.

> So karohi dīpamattano, Khippaṁ vāyama paṇḍito bhava; Niddhantamalo anaṅgaṇo, Na punaṁ jātijaraṁ upehisi.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: all · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [dīpa](../terms/dipa.md), [mala](../terms/mala-pali.md), [jāti](../terms/jati.md), [jarā](../terms/jara.md) · concepts: [Oneself as island, the Dhamma as island](../concepts/attadipa.md)

### 239 <a id="tea-dhammapada-239"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Gradually, little by little, moment by moment, the wise one should blow away his own stains (mala), as a smith (blows away the dross) of silver.

> Aññatarabrāhmaṇavatthu Anupubbena medhāvī, thokaṁ thokaṁ khaṇe khaṇe; Kammāro rajatasseva, niddhame malamattano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [mala](../terms/mala-pali.md)

### 240 <a id="tea-dhammapada-240"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

As rust arising from iron, having arisen from it, eats that very iron, so one's own deeds lead the one who conducts himself in excess (atidhonacārī) to a bad destination (duggati).

> Tissattheravatthu Ayasāva malaṁ samuṭṭhitaṁ, Tatuṭṭhāya tameva khādati; Evaṁ atidhonacārinaṁ, Sāni kammāni nayanti duggatiṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [kamma](../terms/kamma.md), [duggati](../terms/duggati.md), [atidhonacārī](../terms/atidhonacari.md)

### 241 <a id="tea-dhammapada-241"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not reciting (asajjhāya) is the stain of the mantras (mantā); not exerting oneself (anuṭṭhāna) is the stain of houses; sloth (kosajja) is the stain of beauty (vaṇṇa); heedlessness (pamāda) is the stain of a guard.

> Lāḷudāyīvatthu Asajjhāyamalā mantā, anuṭṭhānamalā gharā; Malaṁ vaṇṇassa kosajjaṁ, pamādo rakkhato malaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [mala](../terms/mala-pali.md), [kosajja](../terms/kosajja.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [mantā](../terms/manta.md), [asajjhāya](../terms/asajjhaya.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md), [Sloth, laziness (kosajja)](../obstacles/kosajja.md)

### 242 <a id="tea-dhammapada-242"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Misconduct (duccarita) is the stain of a woman; stinginess (macchera) is the stain of a giver; evil states (pāpakā dhammā) are stains, in this world and the next.

> Aññatarakulaputtavatthu Malitthiyā duccaritaṁ, maccheraṁ dadato malaṁ; Malā ve pāpakā dhammā, asmiṁ loke paramhi ca.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [mala](../terms/mala-pali.md), [duccarita](../terms/duccarita.md), [macchera](../terms/macchera.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md) · obstacles: [Stinginess (macchera)](../obstacles/macchera.md)

### 243 <a id="tea-dhammapada-243"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Worse than these stains is the worst stain, ignorance (avijjā); having abandoned this stain, be stainless, bhikkhus.

> Tato malā malataraṁ, avijjā paramaṁ malaṁ; Etaṁ malaṁ pahantvāna, nimmalā hotha bhikkhavo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [mala](../terms/mala-pali.md), [avijjā](../terms/avijja.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md) · obstacles: [Ignorance (avijjā)](../obstacles/avijja.md)

### 244 <a id="tea-dhammapada-244"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Life is easy for the shameless (ahirika), the crow-hardy (kākasūra), the destructive, the forward, the brazen, the defiled.

> Cūḷasāribhikkhuvatthu Sujīvaṁ ahirikena, kākasūrena dhaṁsinā; Pakkhandinā pagabbhena, saṅkiliṭṭhena jīvitaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [ahirika](../terms/ahirika.md)

### 245 <a id="tea-dhammapada-245"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Life is hard for one who has shame (hirīmant), who is always seeking purity, unattached, not brazen, of pure livelihood, and discerning (passant).

> Hirīmatā ca dujjīvaṁ, niccaṁ sucigavesinā; Alīnenāppagabbhena, suddhājīvena passatā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [hiri](../terms/hiri.md)

### 246 <a id="tea-dhammapada-246"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whoever takes life, speaks falsehood, takes in the world what is not given, and goes to another's wife,

> Pañcaupāsakavatthu Yo pāṇamatipāteti, musāvādañca bhāsati; Loke adinnamādiyati, paradārañca gacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [pāṇātipāta](../terms/panatipata.md), [musāvāda](../terms/musavada.md), [adinnādāna](../terms/adinnadana.md)

### 247 <a id="tea-dhammapada-247"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

and the man who devotes himself to drinking liquor and spirits (surāmeraya): he digs up his own root right here in the world.

> Surāmerayapānañca, yo naro anuyuñjati; Idheva meso lokasmiṁ, mūlaṁ khaṇati attano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [surāmeraya](../terms/surameraya.md)

### 248 <a id="tea-dhammapada-248"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Know this, good man: those of evil states, the unrestrained, (are so); do not let greed (lobha) and unrighteousness (adhamma) torment you for long to suffering.

> Evaṁ bho purisa jānāhi, pāpadhammā asaññatā; Mā taṁ lobho adhammo ca, ciraṁ dukkhāya randhayuṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [lobha](../terms/lobha.md), [adhamma](../terms/adhamma.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md) · obstacles: [Greed (lobha)](../obstacles/lobha.md)

### 249 <a id="tea-dhammapada-249"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

People give according to their faith (saddhā) and their confidence; whoever is discontented (maṅku) about the drink and food of others does not attain concentration (samādhi), by day or by night.

> Tissadaharavatthu Dadāti ve yathāsaddhaṁ, yathāpasādanaṁ jano; Tattha yo ca maṅku bhavati, paresaṁ pānabhojane; Na so divā vā rattiṁ vā, samādhimadhigacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [dāna](../terms/dana.md), [saddhā](../terms/saddha.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md)

### 250 <a id="tea-dhammapada-250"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

But one in whom this is cut off, uprooted, destroyed at the root, does attain concentration, by day and by night.

> Yassa cetaṁ samucchinnaṁ, mūlaghaccaṁ samūhataṁ; Sa ve divā vā rattiṁ vā, samādhimadhigacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md)

### 251 <a id="tea-dhammapada-251"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

There is no fire like passion (rāga), no grip like hatred (dosa), no net like delusion (moha), no river like craving (taṇhā).

> Pañcaupāsakavatthu Natthi rāgasamo aggi, natthi dosasamo gaho; Natthi mohasamaṁ jālaṁ, natthi taṇhāsamā nadī.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [rāga](../terms/raga.md), [doṣa](../terms/dosa.md), [moha](../terms/moha.md), [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md) · obstacles: [Passion (rāga)](../obstacles/raga-pali.md), [Hate (dosa)](../obstacles/dosa.md), [Delusion (moha)](../obstacles/moha.md), [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md)

### 252 <a id="tea-dhammapada-252"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The faults of others are easy to see, one's own are hard to see; one winnows another's faults like chaff, but conceals one's own, as a cheating gambler hides an unlucky throw.

> Meṇḍakaseṭṭhivatthu Sudassaṁ vajjamaññesaṁ, attano pana duddasaṁ; Paresaṁ hi so vajjāni, opunāti yathā bhusaṁ; Attano pana chādeti, kaliṁva kitavā saṭho.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, ethics_

terms: [vajja](../terms/vajja.md)

### 253 <a id="tea-dhammapada-253"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For one who looks at the faults of others, always taking offence (ujjhānasaññī), the taints (āsavā) increase; he is far from the destruction of the taints.

> Ujjhānasaññittheravatthu Paravajjānupassissa, niccaṁ ujjhānasaññino; Āsavā tassa vaḍḍhanti, ārā so āsavakkhayā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [āsava](../terms/asava.md)

### 254 <a id="tea-dhammapada-254"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

There is no track in the sky, and no samaṇa outside; people delight in proliferation (papañca); the Tathāgatas are free of proliferation.

> Subhaddaparibbājakavatthu Ākāseva padaṁ natthi, samaṇo natthi bāhire; Papañcābhiratā pajā, nippapañcā tathāgatā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: realized · types: ultimate_

terms: [śramaṇa](../terms/sramana.md), [papañca](../terms/papanca.md), [tathāgata](../terms/tathagata.md)

### 255 <a id="tea-dhammapada-255"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

There is no track in the sky, and no samaṇa outside; conditioned things (saṅkhārā) are not eternal; there is no wavering (iñjita) in the Buddhas.

> Ākāseva padaṁ natthi, samaṇo natthi bāhire; Saṅkhārā sassatā natthi, natthi buddhānamiñjitaṁ. Malavaggo aṭṭhārasamo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: realized · types: ultimate_

terms: [śramaṇa](../terms/sramana.md), [saṅkhāra](../terms/sankhara.md), [buddha](../terms/buddha.md)

### 256 <a id="tea-dhammapada-256"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One is not established in the Dhamma (dhammaṭṭha) by deciding a matter by force (sāhasā); the wise one would examine both the matter (attha) and what is not the matter (anattha).

> Na tena hoti dhammaṭṭho, yenatthaṁ sāhasā naye; Yo ca atthaṁ anatthañca, ubho niccheyya paṇḍito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [dhammaṭṭha](../terms/dhammattha.md), [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [attha](../terms/attha.md)

### 257 <a id="tea-dhammapada-257"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who leads others without force, by Dhamma, evenly, the wise one guarded by the Dhamma, is called 'established in the Dhamma' (dhammaṭṭha).

> Asāhasena dhammena, samena nayatī pare; Dhammassa gutto medhāvī, “dhammaṭṭho”ti pavuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [dhammaṭṭha](../terms/dhammattha.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md)

### 258 <a id="tea-dhammapada-258"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One is not wise (paṇḍita) merely by speaking much; one who is secure (khemī), without enmity (averī) and fearless (abhaya) is called wise.

> Chabbaggiyavatthu Na tena paṇḍito hoti, yāvatā bahu bhāsati; Khemī averī abhayo, “paṇḍito”ti pavuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [avera](../terms/avera.md), [bhaya](../terms/bhaya.md)

### 259 <a id="tea-dhammapada-259"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One is not a Dhamma-holder (dhammadhara) merely by speaking much; one who, having heard even a little, sees the Dhamma with the body and does not neglect the Dhamma is indeed a Dhamma-holder.

> Ekudānakhīṇāsavavatthu Na tāvatā dhammadharo, yāvatā bahu bhāsati; Yo ca appampi sutvāna, dhammaṁ kāyena passati; Sa ve dhammadharo hoti, yo dhammaṁ nappamajjati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [dhammadhara](../terms/dhammadhara.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md)

### 260 <a id="tea-dhammapada-260"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One is not an elder (thera) because his head is grey; if his age is ripe he is called 'grown old in vain' (moghajiṇṇa).

> Lakuṇḍakabhaddiyattheravatthu Na tena thero so hoti, yenassa palitaṁ siro; Paripakko vayo tassa, “moghajiṇṇo”ti vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [thera](../terms/thera.md)

### 261 <a id="tea-dhammapada-261"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

In whom there are truth (sacca), Dhamma, non-harming (ahiṁsā), restraint (saṃyama) and self-control (dama): that steadfast one, who has vomited out the stain, is called an elder (thera).

> Yamhi saccañca dhammo ca, ahiṁsā saṁyamo damo; Sa ve vantamalo dhīro, “thero” iti pavuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: realized · types: ethics_

terms: [thera](../terms/thera.md), [sacca](../terms/sacca.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [ahiṃsā](../terms/ahimsa-pali.md), [saṃyama](../terms/samyama-pali.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md), [mala](../terms/mala-pali.md) · practices: [Non-harming (ahiṃsā)](../practices/ahimsa-pali.md)

### 262 <a id="tea-dhammapada-262"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not by mere speech, nor by beauty of complexion, does a person become of good appearance (sādhurūpa) if he is envious (issukī), stingy (maccharī) and deceitful (saṭha).

> Sambahulabhikkhuvatthu Na vākkaraṇamattena, vaṇṇapokkharatāya vā; Sādhurūpo naro hoti, issukī maccharī saṭho.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sādhurūpa](../terms/sadhurupa.md), [issukī](../terms/issuki.md), [maccharī](../terms/maccharin.md), [saṭha](../terms/satha.md) · obstacles: [Stinginess (macchera)](../obstacles/macchera.md), [Envy (issā)](../obstacles/issa.md)

### 263 <a id="tea-dhammapada-263"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One in whom this is cut off, uprooted, destroyed at the root, and who has spat out hatred (vantadosa), the wise one, is called 'of good appearance'.

> Yassa cetaṁ samucchinnaṁ, mūlaghaccaṁ samūhataṁ; Sa vantadoso medhāvī, “sādhurūpo”ti vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sādhurūpa](../terms/sadhurupa.md), [doṣa](../terms/dosa.md)

### 264 <a id="tea-dhammapada-264"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not by a shaven head is one a samaṇa, if without vows (abbata) and speaking falsehood; filled with desire and greed (icchalobha), how could he be a samaṇa?

> Hatthakavatthu Na muṇḍakena samaṇo, abbato alikaṁ bhaṇaṁ; Icchālobhasamāpanno, samaṇo kiṁ bhavissati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [śramaṇa](../terms/sramana.md), [icchālobha](../terms/icchalobha.md) · obstacles: [Greed (lobha)](../obstacles/lobha.md)

### 265 <a id="tea-dhammapada-265"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who calms (sameti) the evils, small and large, in every way, is called a samaṇa because of the calming of evils.

> Yo ca sameti pāpāni, aṇuṁthūlāni sabbaso; Samitattā hi pāpānaṁ, “samaṇo”ti pavuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: realized · types: ethics_

terms: [śramaṇa](../terms/sramana.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md) · concepts: [Śramaṇa and brāhmaṇa](../concepts/sramana-brahmana-contrast.md)

### 266 <a id="tea-dhammapada-266"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One is not a bhikkhu merely by begging from others; having taken up a vissa practice (vissaṁ dhammaṁ), one is not thereby a bhikkhu.

> Aññatarabrāhmaṇavatthu Na tena bhikkhu so hoti, yāvatā bhikkhate pare; Vissaṁ dhammaṁ samādāya, bhikkhu hoti na tāvatā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md)

### 267 <a id="tea-dhammapada-267"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Here, one who has set aside merit and evil (puñña, pāpa), who leads the holy life (brahmacariyavā) and goes about in the world with discernment (saṅkhāya), is indeed called a bhikkhu.

> Yodha puññañca pāpañca, bāhetvā brahmacariyavā; Saṅkhāya loke carati, sa ve “bhikkhū”ti vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: realized · types: ethics_

terms: [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [brahmacariya](../terms/brahmacariya.md)

### 268 <a id="tea-dhammapada-268"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not by silence (mona) does a deluded, ignorant person become a sage (muni); but the wise one who, as if taking up a scale, takes up the best,

> Titthiyavatthu Na monena munī hoti, mūḷharūpo aviddasu; Yo ca tulaṁva paggayha, varamādāya paṇḍito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [muni](../terms/muni.md), [mona](../terms/mona.md)

### 269 <a id="tea-dhammapada-269"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

and avoids evils: he is a sage, and by that a sage; one who 'measures' (munāti) both worlds is called a sage on that account.

> Pāpāni parivajjeti, sa munī tena so muni; Yo munāti ubho loke, “muni” tena pavuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [muni](../terms/muni.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md)

### 270 <a id="tea-dhammapada-270"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One is not noble (ariya) by harming living beings; by non-harming of all beings one is called noble.

> Bālisikavatthu Na tena ariyo hoti, yena pāṇāni hiṁsati; Ahiṁsā sabbapāṇānaṁ, “ariyo”ti pavuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [ariya](../terms/ariya.md), [ahiṃsā](../terms/ahimsa-pali.md) · practices: [Non-harming (ahiṃsā)](../practices/ahimsa-pali.md)

### 271 <a id="tea-dhammapada-271"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Not by mere virtue and vows (sīlabbata), nor by much learning (bāhusacca), nor by the attainment of concentration (samādhilābha), nor by solitary lodging (vivittasayana),

> Sambahulasīlādisampannabhikkhuvatthu Na sīlabbatamattena, bāhusaccena vā pana; Atha vā samādhilābhena, vivittasayanena vā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: advanced · types: practice_

terms: [sīlabbata](../terms/silabbata.md), [bāhusacca](../terms/bahusacca.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md), [vivittasayana](../terms/vivittasayana.md)

### 272 <a id="tea-dhammapada-272"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

(does one think) 'I touch the happiness of renunciation (nekkhammasukha), not experienced by ordinary people (puthujjana)': bhikkhu, do not settle into confidence (vissāsa) while you have not reached the destruction of the taints (āsavakkhaya).

> Phusāmi nekkhammasukhaṁ, aputhujjanasevitaṁ; Bhikkhu vissāsamāpādi, appatto āsavakkhayaṁ. Dhammaṭṭhavaggo ekūnavīsatimo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: advanced · types: practice_

terms: [nekkhamma](../terms/nekkhamma.md), [puthujjana](../terms/puthujjana.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [vissāsa](../terms/vissasa.md), [āsavakkhaya](../terms/asavakkhaya.md)

### 273 <a id="tea-dhammapada-273"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Of paths the eightfold is best; of truths the four sayings (padā); dispassion (virāga) is the best of states (dhammā); of two-footed beings the one with vision (cakkhumā) is best.

> Maggānaṭṭhaṅgiko seṭṭho, saccānaṁ caturo padā; Virāgo seṭṭho dhammānaṁ, dvipadānañca cakkhumā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ultimate_

terms: [ariya aṭṭhaṅgika magga](../terms/ariya-atthangika-magga.md), [ariyasacca](../terms/ariyasacca.md), [virāga](../terms/viraga.md), [cakkhumā](../terms/cakkhuma.md) · concepts: [The noble eightfold path (ariya aṭṭhaṅgika magga)](../concepts/noble-eightfold-path.md), [The four noble truths (cattāri ariyasaccāni)](../concepts/four-noble-truths.md)

### 274 <a id="tea-dhammapada-274"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This is the path, there is no other, for the purification of vision (dassana); follow it; this is what bewilders Māra.

> Eseva maggo natthañño, Dassanassa visuddhiyā; Etañhi tumhe paṭipajjatha, Mārassetaṁ pamohanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [dassana](../terms/dassana.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 275 <a id="tea-dhammapada-275"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Having followed this path you will make an end of suffering (dukkha); the path was declared by me, having understood the removal of the dart (sallakantana).

> Etañhi tumhe paṭipannā, dukkhassantaṁ karissatha; Akkhāto vo mayā maggo, aññāya sallakantanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md), [sallakantana](../terms/sallakantana.md)

### 276 <a id="tea-dhammapada-276"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

You yourselves must make the effort (ātappa); the Tathāgatas are only teachers (akkhātāro); those who practise, the meditators (jhāyī), are freed from Māra's bonds (mārabandhana).

> Tumhehi kiccamātappaṁ, akkhātāro tathāgatā; Paṭipannā pamokkhanti, jhāyino mārabandhanā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: practice, teacher-transmission_

terms: [ātappa](../terms/atappa.md), [tathāgata](../terms/tathagata.md), [jhāyī](../terms/jhayin.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [bandhana](../terms/bandhana.md) · obstacles: [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 277 <a id="tea-dhammapada-277"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'All formations (saṅkhārā) are impermanent (aniccā)': when one sees this with wisdom (paññā), then one becomes disenchanted (nibbindati) with suffering; this is the path to purification.

> Aniccalakkhaṇavatthu “Sabbe saṅkhārā aniccā”ti, yadā paññāya passati; Atha nibbindati dukkhe, esa maggo visuddhiyā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: knowledge · stage: advanced · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [saṅkhāra](../terms/sankhara.md), [anicca](../terms/anicca.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [nibbidā](../terms/nibbida.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md) · concepts: [The three marks](../concepts/three-marks.md)

### 277-279 <a id="tea-dhammapada-277-279"></a>
`skeleton` · confidence high

'All formations are impermanent', 'all formations are suffering', 'all phenomena are not-self': when one sees this with wisdom, one becomes disenchanted with suffering — this is the path to purity.

> “Sabbe saṅkhārā aniccā”ti, yadā paññāya passati; Atha nibbindati dukkhe, esa maggo visuddhiyā. “Sabbe saṅkhārā dukkhā”ti, yadā paññāya passati; Atha nibbindati dukkhe, esa maggo visuddhiyā. “Sabbe dhammā anattā”ti, yadā paññāya passati; Atha nibbindati dukkhe, esa maggo visuddhiyā.

_level: ultimate · standpoint: analytic · path: knowledge · stage: all · types: ultimate, practice_

'All formations (saṅkhārā) are suffering (dukkhā)': when one sees this with wisdom, then one becomes disenchanted with suffering; this is the path to purification.

> Dukkhalakkhaṇavatthu “Sabbe saṅkhārā dukkhā”ti, yadā paññāya passati; Atha nibbindati dukkhe, esa maggo visuddhiyā.

### 279 <a id="tea-dhammapada-279"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'All dhammas are not-self (anattā)': when one sees this with wisdom, then one becomes disenchanted with suffering; this is the path to purification.

> Anattalakkhaṇavatthu “Sabbe dhammā anattā”ti, yadā paññāya passati; Atha nibbindati dukkhe, esa maggo visuddhiyā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: knowledge · stage: advanced · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

### 280 <a id="tea-dhammapada-280"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who does not rise at the time for rising, young and strong but given to laziness, with a mind sunk in thoughts (saṁsannasaṅkappamana), slack (kusīta) and lazy, does not find the path by wisdom.

> Padhānakammikatissattheravatthu Uṭṭhānakālamhi anuṭṭhahāno, Yuvā balī ālasiyaṁ upeto; Saṁsannasaṅkappamano kusīto, Paññāya maggaṁ alaso na vindati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [ālasiya](../terms/alasiya.md), [kusīta](../terms/kusita.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [saṅkappa](../terms/sankappa.md) · obstacles: [Sloth, laziness (kosajja)](../obstacles/kosajja.md)

### 281 <a id="tea-dhammapada-281"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Guarding speech, well restrained in mind, one should do nothing unskilful (akusala) with the body; one should purify these three courses of action (kammapatha) and attain the path taught by the seers (isi).

> Sūkarapetavatthu Vācānurakkhī manasā susaṁvuto, Kāyena ca nākusalaṁ kayirā; Ete tayo kammapathe visodhaye, Ārādhaye maggamisippaveditaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [vācā](../terms/vaca.md), [mano](../terms/mano.md), [kāya](../terms/kaya.md), [akusala](../terms/akusala.md), [kammapatha](../terms/kammapatha.md), [isi](../terms/isi.md), [magga](../terms/magga.md)

### 282 <a id="tea-dhammapada-282"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

From application (yoga) wisdom (bhūri) arises; from non-application wisdom is destroyed; knowing this twofold path of gain and loss (bhavāya vibhavāya), one should so direct oneself that wisdom grows.

> Poṭṭhilattheravatthu Yogā ve jāyatī bhūri, ayogā bhūrisaṅkhayo; Etaṁ dvedhāpathaṁ ñatvā, bhavāya vibhavāya ca; Tathāttānaṁ niveseyya, yathā bhūri pavaḍḍhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [yoga](../terms/yoga-pali.md), [bhūri](../terms/bhuri.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md)

### 283 <a id="tea-dhammapada-283"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Cut down the forest (vana), not the tree; fear (bhaya) arises from the forest; having cut down the forest and the undergrowth (vanatha), be forestless (nibbana), bhikkhus.

> Pañcamahallakabhikkhuvatthu Vanaṁ chindatha mā rukkhaṁ, vanato jāyate bhayaṁ; Chetvā vanañca vanathañca, nibbanā hotha bhikkhavo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [vana](../terms/vana.md), [vanatha](../terms/vanatha.md), [bhaya](../terms/bhaya.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md)

### 284 <a id="tea-dhammapada-284"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As long as even the smallest undergrowth (vanatha) of a man for women is not cut, so long is his mind bound, like a suckling calf to its mother.

> Yāva hi vanatho na chijjati, Aṇumattopi narassa nārisu; Paṭibaddhamanova tāva so, Vaccho khīrapakova mātari.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [vanatha](../terms/vanatha.md)

### 285 <a id="tea-dhammapada-285"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Cut off your own affection (sineha), as one plucks an autumn lily (kumuda) with the hand; foster only the path of peace (santimagga), nibbāna taught by the Well-gone (sugata).

> Suvaṇṇakārattheravatthu Ucchinda sinehamattano, Kumudaṁ sāradikaṁva pāṇinā; Santimaggameva brūhaya, Nibbānaṁ sugatena desitaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [sineha](../terms/sineha.md), [santimagga](../terms/santimagga.md), [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md), [sugata](../terms/sugata-pali.md) · concepts: [Nibbāna](../concepts/nibbana.md)

### 286 <a id="tea-dhammapada-286"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'Here I shall spend the rains, here winter and summer': so the fool thinks, and does not realise the danger (antarāya).

> Mahādhanavāṇijavatthu Idha vassaṁ vasissāmi, idha hemantagimhisu; Iti bālo vicinteti, antarāyaṁ na bujjhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [antarāya](../terms/antaraya-pali.md)

### 287 <a id="tea-dhammapada-287"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Death carries off the man intoxicated with sons and cattle, his mind clinging, as a great flood carries off a sleeping village.

> Kisāgotamīvatthu Taṁ puttapasusammattaṁ, byāsattamanasaṁ naraṁ; Suttaṁ gāmaṁ mahoghova, maccu ādāya gacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [maccu](../terms/maccu.md)

### 288 <a id="tea-dhammapada-288"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Sons are no protection, nor a father, nor relatives; for one overcome by the Ender (antaka) there is no protection among kin.

> Paṭācārāvatthu Na santi puttā tāṇāya, na pitā nāpi bandhavā; Antakenādhipannassa, natthi ñātīsu tāṇatā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: death-dying_

terms: [antaka](../terms/antaka.md)

### 289 <a id="tea-dhammapada-289"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Knowing this reason, the wise one, restrained by virtue, should quickly purify the path that leads to nibbāna.

> Etamatthavasaṁ ñatvā, paṇḍito sīlasaṁvuto; Nibbānagamanaṁ maggaṁ, khippameva visodhaye. Maggavaggo vīsatimo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [paṇḍita](../terms/pandita-pali.md), [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [nibbāna](../terms/nibbana.md) · concepts: [Nibbāna](../concepts/nibbana.md)

### 290 <a id="tea-dhammapada-290"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If by giving up a small pleasure (mattāsukha) one would see a great pleasure, the steadfast one should give up the small pleasure, seeing the great.

> Mattāsukhapariccāgā, passe ce vipulaṁ sukhaṁ; Caje mattāsukhaṁ dhīro, sampassaṁ vipulaṁ sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [dhīra](../terms/dhira-pali.md)

### 291 <a id="tea-dhammapada-291"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who wants his own happiness by causing pain to others, entangled in the bonds of enmity (vera), is not freed from enmity.

> Kukkuṭaṇḍakhādikāvatthu Paradukkhūpadhānena, attano sukhamicchati; Verasaṁsaggasaṁsaṭṭho, verā so na parimuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [vera](../terms/vera.md)

### 292 <a id="tea-dhammapada-292"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

What should be done is thrown aside and what should not be done is done: for those who are arrogant (unnaḷa) and heedless, the taints (āsavā) increase.

> Bhaddiyānaṁbhikkhūnaṁvatthu Yañhi kiccaṁ apaviddhaṁ, akiccaṁ pana karīyati; Unnaḷānaṁ pamattānaṁ, tesaṁ vaḍḍhanti āsavā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [āsava](../terms/asava.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md) · obstacles: [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 293 <a id="tea-dhammapada-293"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Those who constantly have mindfulness directed to the body (kāyagatā sati) well established, who do not practise what should not be done and are steady in doing what should be done — for those mindful and clearly comprehending, the taints (āsavā) come to an end.

> Yesañca susamāraddhā, niccaṁ kāyagatā sati; Akiccaṁ te na sevanti, kicce sātaccakārino; Satānaṁ sampajānānaṁ, atthaṁ gacchanti āsavā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [kāyagatāsati](../terms/kayagatasati.md), [sati](../terms/sati.md), [sampajāna](../terms/sampajana.md), [āsava](../terms/asava.md) · practices: [Mindfulness directed to the body (kāyagatāsati): summary only](../practices/kayagatasati.md)

### 294 <a id="tea-dhammapada-294"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Having killed mother and father, and two kings of the warrior class, and having killed a realm with its followers, the brāhmaṇa goes on free of distress (anīgha).

> Lakuṇḍakabhaddiyavatthu Mātaraṁ pitaraṁ hantvā, rājāno dve ca khattiye; Raṭṭhaṁ sānucaraṁ hantvā, anīgho yāti brāhmaṇo.

_level: unmarked · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: realized · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

### 295 <a id="tea-dhammapada-295"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Having killed mother and father, and two learned (sotthiya) kings, and having killed the tiger as the fifth (veyyagghapañcama), the brāhmaṇa goes on free of distress (anīgha).

> Mātaraṁ pitaraṁ hantvā, rājāno dve ca sotthiye; Veyagghapañcamaṁ hantvā, anīgho yāti brāhmaṇo.

_level: unmarked · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: realized · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [brāhmaṇa](../terms/brahmana.md), [anīgha](../terms/anigha.md)

### 296 <a id="tea-dhammapada-296"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The disciples of Gotama always wake up well (suppabuddha) — those whose mindfulness, by day and by night, is constantly directed to the Buddha (buddhagatā sati).

> Dārusākaṭikaputtavatthu Suppabuddhaṁ pabujjhanti, sadā gotamasāvakā; Yesaṁ divā ca ratto ca, niccaṁ buddhagatā sati.

### 300 <a id="tea-dhammapada-300"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The disciples of Gotama always wake up well — those whose mind, by day and by night, delights in non-harming (ahiṁsā).

> Suppabuddhaṁ pabujjhanti, sadā gotamasāvakā; Yesaṁ divā ca ratto ca, ahiṁsāya rato mano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, ethics_

### 301 <a id="tea-dhammapada-301"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

The disciples of Gotama always wake up well — those whose mind, by day and by night, delights in development (bhāvanā).

> Suppabuddhaṁ pabujjhanti, sadā gotamasāvakā; Yesaṁ divā ca ratto ca, bhāvanāya rato mano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

### 302 <a id="tea-dhammapada-302"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Hard is going forth (pabbajjā), hard to delight in; hard, painful are household dwellings; painful is living with those unlike oneself; painful for the wanderer (addhagū) is what befalls him: therefore let one not be a wanderer, nor one on whom suffering falls.

> Vajjiputtakabhikkhuvatthu Duppabbajjaṁ durabhiramaṁ, Durāvāsā gharā dukhā; Dukkhosamānasaṁvāso, Dukkhānupatitaddhagū; Tasmā na caddhagū siyā, Na ca dukkhānupatito siyā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [pabbajjā](../terms/pabbajja.md), [addhagū](../terms/addhagu.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 303 <a id="tea-dhammapada-303"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who has faith (saddhā), is endowed with virtue (sīla), and possesses fame and wealth is honoured wherever he goes.

> Cittagahapativatthu Saddho sīlena sampanno, yasobhogasamappito; Yaṁ yaṁ padesaṁ bhajati, tattha tattheva pūjito.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [saddhā](../terms/saddha.md), [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [yasa](../terms/yasa.md)

### 304 <a id="tea-dhammapada-304"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The good shine from afar, like the Himalaya mountains; the bad are not seen here, like arrows shot in the night.

> Cūḷasubhaddāvatthu Dūre santo pakāsenti, himavantova pabbato; Asantettha na dissanti, rattiṁ khittā yathā sarā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [santa](../terms/santa-pali.md), [asanta](../terms/asanta.md)

### 305 <a id="tea-dhammapada-305"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Sitting alone, sleeping alone, faring alone, unwearied, taming himself alone, he would find delight at the forest's edge.

> Ekavihārittheravatthu Ekāsanaṁ ekaseyyaṁ, eko caramatandito; Eko damayamattānaṁ, vanante ramito siyā. Pakiṇṇakavaggo ekavīsatimo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [eka](../terms/eka.md), [attā](../terms/atta.md), [dama](../terms/dama-pali.md) · practices: [Solitary living (ekacariya)](../practices/ekacariya.md)

### 306 <a id="tea-dhammapada-306"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who speaks what is not so (abhūtavādī) goes to hell (niraya), and so does one who, having done a deed, says 'I did not do it'; after death both become alike, men of base deeds in the next world.

> Abhūtavādī nirayaṁ upeti, Yo vāpi katvā na karomi cāha; Ubhopi te pecca samā bhavanti, Nihīnakammā manujā parattha.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, ethics_

terms: [abhūtavādī](../terms/abhutavadi.md), [niraya](../terms/niraya.md)

### 307 <a id="tea-dhammapada-307"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Many with the ochre robe around their necks are of evil states and unrestrained; the evil, by their evil deeds, are reborn in hell.

> Duccaritaphalapīḷitavatthu Kāsāvakaṇṭhā bahavo, pāpadhammā asaññatā; Pāpā pāpehi kammehi, nirayaṁ te upapajjare.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, world-fate_

terms: [kasāva](../terms/kasava.md), [niraya](../terms/niraya.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md)

### 308 <a id="tea-dhammapada-308"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better to have swallowed a heated iron ball, like a flame of fire, than that an immoral (dussīla), unrestrained person should eat the country's alms-food (raṭṭhapiṇḍa).

> Vaggumudātīriyabhikkhuvatthu Seyyo ayoguḷo bhutto, tatto aggisikhūpamo; Yañce bhuñjeyya dussīlo, raṭṭhapiṇḍamasaññato.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [dussīla](../terms/dussila.md)

### 309 <a id="tea-dhammapada-309"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

A heedless man who consorts with another's wife meets four things: gaining demerit (apuñña), disturbed sleep (na nikāmaseyya), blame third, hell (niraya) fourth.

> Khemakaseṭṭhiputtavatthu Cattāri ṭhānāni naro pamatto, Āpajjati paradārūpasevī; Apuññalābhaṁ na nikāmaseyyaṁ, Nindaṁ tatīyaṁ nirayaṁ catutthaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md), [niraya](../terms/niraya.md), [apuñña](../terms/apunna.md)

### 310 <a id="tea-dhammapada-310"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Gaining demerit and a bad destination, the brief delight of a frightened man and a frightened woman, and the king imposes a heavy penalty: therefore a man should not consort with another's wife.

> Apuññalābho ca gatī ca pāpikā, Bhītassa bhītāya ratī ca thokikā; Rājā ca daṇḍaṁ garukaṁ paṇeti, Tasmā naro paradāraṁ na seve.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [rati](../terms/rati-pali.md)

### 311 <a id="tea-dhammapada-311"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As kusa grass wrongly grasped cuts the hand itself, so the ascetic life (sāmañña) wrongly handled drags one down to hell.

> Dubbacabhikkhuvatthu Kuso yathā duggahito, hatthamevānukantati; Sāmaññaṁ dupparāmaṭṭhaṁ, nirayāyupakaḍḍhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sāmañña](../terms/samanna.md), [niraya](../terms/niraya.md)

### 312 <a id="tea-dhammapada-312"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Any lax action, any defiled vow, any hesitant (saṅkassara) holy life: that is not of great fruit.

> Yaṁ kiñci sithilaṁ kammaṁ, saṅkiliṭṭhañca yaṁ vataṁ; Saṅkassaraṁ brahmacariyaṁ, na taṁ hoti mahapphalaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [brahmacariya](../terms/brahmacariya.md)

### 313 <a id="tea-dhammapada-313"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

If something is to be done, one should do it and press on firmly; for a lax wandering life (paribbāja) scatters yet more dust.

> Kayirā ce kayirāthenaṁ, daḷhamenaṁ parakkame; Sithilo hi paribbājo, bhiyyo ākirate rajaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [paribbājaka](../terms/paribbajaka.md)

### 314 <a id="tea-dhammapada-314"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better a bad deed (dukkaṭa) not done, for a bad deed torments afterwards; better a good deed (sukata) done, which one does not regret after doing.

> Issāpakataitthivatthu Akataṁ dukkaṭaṁ seyyo, pacchā tappati dukkaṭaṁ; Katañca sukataṁ seyyo, yaṁ katvā nānutappati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

### 315 <a id="tea-dhammapada-315"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a border city is guarded inside and out, so guard yourselves; do not let the moment (khaṇa) pass you by; those for whom the moment has passed grieve, consigned to hell.

> Sambahulabhikkhuvatthu Nagaraṁ yathā paccantaṁ, guttaṁ santarabāhiraṁ; Evaṁ gopetha attānaṁ, khaṇo vo mā upaccagā; Khaṇātītā hi socanti, nirayamhi samappitā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [khaṇa](../terms/khana.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md), [niraya](../terms/niraya.md), [attā](../terms/atta.md)

### 316 <a id="tea-dhammapada-316"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

They are ashamed of what is not shameful and not ashamed of what is shameful; by taking up wrong view (micchādiṭṭhi), beings go to a bad destination (duggati).

> Nigaṇṭhavatthu Alajjitāye lajjanti, lajjitāye na lajjare; Micchādiṭṭhisamādānā, sattā gacchanti duggatiṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [micchādiṭṭhi](../terms/micchaditthi.md), [duggati](../terms/duggati.md)

### 317 <a id="tea-dhammapada-317"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

They see danger where there is none and do not see danger where there is; by taking up wrong view, beings go to a bad destination.

> Abhaye bhayadassino, bhaye cābhayadassino; Micchādiṭṭhisamādānā, sattā gacchanti duggatiṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [micchādiṭṭhi](../terms/micchaditthi.md), [duggati](../terms/duggati.md), [bhaya](../terms/bhaya.md)

### 318 <a id="tea-dhammapada-318"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

They see fault in what is faultless and do not see it in what is at fault; by taking up wrong view, beings go to a bad destination.

> Titthiyasāvakavatthu Avajje vajjamatino, vajje cāvajjadassino; Micchādiṭṭhisamādānā, sattā gacchanti duggatiṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [micchādiṭṭhi](../terms/micchaditthi.md), [duggati](../terms/duggati.md), [vajja](../terms/vajja.md)

### 319 <a id="tea-dhammapada-319"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Knowing fault as fault and the faultless as faultless, by taking up right view (sammādiṭṭhi), beings go to a good destination (suggati).

> Vajjañca vajjato ñatvā, avajjañca avajjato; Sammādiṭṭhisamādānā, sattā gacchanti suggatiṁ. Nirayavaggo dvāvīsatimo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [sammādiṭṭhi](../terms/sammaditthi.md), [suggati](../terms/suggati.md), [vajja](../terms/vajja.md)

### 320 <a id="tea-dhammapada-320"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'I shall endure abuse (ativākya) as an elephant in battle endures the arrow that has fallen from the bow; for the many are ill-behaved (dussīla).'

> Ahaṁ nāgova saṅgāme, cāpato patitaṁ saraṁ; Ativākyaṁ titikkhissaṁ, dussīlo hi bahujjano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [titikkhā](../terms/titikkha.md), [dussīla](../terms/dussila.md)

### 321 <a id="tea-dhammapada-321"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The tamed they lead into the assembly; the king mounts the tamed; the tamed one is the best among human beings, the one who endures abuse.

> Dantaṁ nayanti samitiṁ, dantaṁ rājābhirūhati; Danto seṭṭho manussesu, yotivākyaṁ titikkhati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [danta](../terms/danta.md)

### 322 <a id="tea-dhammapada-322"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better than well-tamed mules, than thoroughbred Sindh horses, than great tusker elephants, is the self-tamed (attadanta).

> Varamassatarā dantā, ājānīyā ca sindhavā; Kuñjarā ca mahānāgā, attadanto tato varaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md), [danta](../terms/danta.md)

### 323 <a id="tea-dhammapada-323"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For by these vehicles one would not go to the untravelled region (agataṁ disaṁ) as the tamed one goes, by the self that is well tamed.

> Hatthācariyapubbakabhikkhuvatthu Na hi etehi yānehi, gaccheyya agataṁ disaṁ; Yathāttanā sudantena, danto dantena gacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [attā](../terms/atta.md), [danta](../terms/danta.md)

### 324 <a id="tea-dhammapada-324"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

The elephant named Dhanapālaka, in rut, hard to hold back, when bound does not eat his mouthfuls; the elephant remembers the elephant-forest.

> Parijiṇṇabrāhmaṇaputtavatthu Dhanapālo nāma kuñjaro, Kaṭukabhedano dunnivārayo; Baddho kabaḷaṁ na bhuñjati, Sumarati nāgavanassa kuñjaro.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

### 325 <a id="tea-dhammapada-325"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

When one is sluggish (middhī), a great eater, a sleeper who rolls about, like a great hog fattened on feed, the dull one comes again and again to the womb.

> Pasenadikosalavatthu Middhī yadā hoti mahagghaso ca, Niddāyitā samparivattasāyī; Mahāvarāhova nivāpapuṭṭho, Punappunaṁ gabbhamupeti mando.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [middha](../terms/middha.md), [gabbha](../terms/gabbha.md)

### 326 <a id="tea-dhammapada-326"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This mind formerly wandered as it liked, where it wished, as it pleased; now I shall restrain it thoroughly (yoniso), as the holder of the goad (does) an elephant in rut.

> Sānusāmaṇeravatthu Idaṁ pure cittamacāri cārikaṁ, Yenicchakaṁ yatthakāmaṁ yathāsukhaṁ; Tadajjahaṁ niggahessāmi yoniso, Hatthippabhinnaṁ viya aṅkusaggaho.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [citta](../terms/citta.md), [niggaha](../terms/niggaha.md), [yoniso](../terms/yoniso.md)

### 327 <a id="tea-dhammapada-327"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Delight in heedfulness (appamāda), guard your own mind (sacittaṁ anurakkhatha); lift yourselves out of the difficult place, like an elephant sunk in mud.

> Pāveyyakahatthivatthu Appamādaratā hotha, sacittamanurakkhatha; Duggā uddharathattānaṁ, paṅke sannova kuñjaro.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [appamāda](../terms/appamada.md), [citta](../terms/citta.md) · practices: [Guarding the mind (cittaṁ rakkhetha)](../practices/citta-rakkha.md)

### 328 <a id="tea-dhammapada-328"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If one gets a wise companion, one who fares with one, is of good conduct and steadfast, overcoming all dangers (parissaya), one should fare with him, glad-minded and mindful (satīmant).

> Sambahulabhikkhuvatthu Sace labhetha nipakaṁ sahāyaṁ, Saddhiṁ caraṁ sādhuvihāridhīraṁ; Abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni, Careyya tenattamano satīmā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [sahāya](../terms/sahaya.md), [parissaya](../terms/parissaya.md), [sati](../terms/sati.md)

### 329 <a id="tea-dhammapada-329"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

If one does not get such a companion, then, like a king leaving a conquered realm, one should fare alone, like the elephant in the elephant forest.

> No ce labhetha nipakaṁ sahāyaṁ, Saddhiṁ caraṁ sādhuvihāridhīraṁ; Rājāva raṭṭhaṁ vijitaṁ pahāya, Eko care mātaṅgaraññeva nāgo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: practice_

terms: [eka](../terms/eka.md) · practices: [Solitary living (ekacariya)](../practices/ekacariya.md)

### 330 <a id="tea-dhammapada-330"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Better to fare alone; there is no companionship with a fool; one should fare alone, do no evil, with few concerns (appossukka), like the elephant in the elephant forest.

> Ekassa caritaṁ seyyo, Natthi bāle sahāyatā; Eko care na ca pāpāni kayirā, Appossukko mātaṅgaraññeva nāgo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: all · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [eka](../terms/eka.md), [bāla](../terms/bala-fool.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md) · practices: [Solitary living (ekacariya)](../practices/ekacariya.md)

### 331 <a id="tea-dhammapada-331"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Friends are pleasant when a need arises; contentment (tuṭṭhī) is pleasant, each with whatever there is; merit (puñña) is pleasant at the ending of life; the abandoning of all suffering is pleasant.

> Māravatthu Atthamhi jātamhi sukhā sahāyā, Tuṭṭhī sukhā yā itarītarena; Puññaṁ sukhaṁ jīvitasaṅkhayamhi, Sabbassa dukkhassa sukhaṁ pahānaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [tuṭṭhī](../terms/tutthi.md), [puñña](../terms/punna-pali.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 332 <a id="tea-dhammapada-332"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Pleasant in the world is service of the mother, pleasant too service of the father; pleasant is service of the samaṇa, and pleasant too service of the brāhmaṇa.

> Sukhā matteyyatā loke, atho petteyyatā sukhā; Sukhā sāmaññatā loke, atho brahmaññatā sukhā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: ethical-social · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics_

terms: [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [matteyyatā](../terms/matteyyata.md), [petteyyatā](../terms/petteyyata.md), [brahmaññatā](../terms/brahmannata.md)

### 333 <a id="tea-dhammapada-333"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Pleasant is virtue (sīla) until old age; pleasant is faith (saddhā) firmly established; pleasant is the gaining of wisdom (paññā); pleasant is the not doing of evils (pāpa).

> Sukhaṁ yāva jarā sīlaṁ, sukhā saddhā patiṭṭhitā; Sukho paññāya paṭilābho, pāpānaṁ akaraṇaṁ sukhaṁ. Nāgavaggo tevīsatimo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, practice_

terms: [sīla](../terms/sila.md), [saddhā](../terms/saddha.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md), [pāpa](../terms/papa-pali.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md)

### 334 <a id="tea-dhammapada-334"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For a person who lives heedlessly (pamattacārī), craving (taṇhā) grows like a māluvā creeper; he leaps from one existence to another (hurā huraṁ), like a monkey in the forest seeking fruit.

> Manujassa pamattacārino, Taṇhā vaḍḍhati māluvā viya; So plavatī hurā huraṁ, Phalamicchaṁva vanasmi vānaro.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [pamāda](../terms/pamada.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md), [Heedlessness (pamāda)](../obstacles/pamada.md)

### 335 <a id="tea-dhammapada-335"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whomever this wretched craving (taṇhā jammī), clinging (visattikā) in the world, overcomes, his sorrows (sokā) grow, like bīraṇa grass well watered by rain.

> Yaṁ esā sahate jammī, taṇhā loke visattikā; Sokā tassa pavaḍḍhanti, abhivaṭṭhaṁva bīraṇaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md), [visattikā](../terms/visattika.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md), [Grief (soka)](../obstacles/soka-pali.md)

### 336 <a id="tea-dhammapada-336"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whoever overcomes this wretched craving, hard to get past (duraccaya) in the world, from him sorrows fall away like a drop of water from a lotus leaf.

> Yo cetaṁ sahate jammiṁ, taṇhaṁ loke duraccayaṁ; Sokā tamhā papatanti, udabinduva pokkharā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: advanced · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [soka](../terms/soka.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md), [Grief (soka)](../obstacles/soka-pali.md)

### 337 <a id="tea-dhammapada-337"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This I tell you — good fortune to you, as many as are gathered here: dig up the root of craving, as one seeking usīra root digs up bīraṇa grass; let Māra not break you again and again, as a stream breaks a reed.

> Taṁ vo vadāmi bhaddaṁ vo, yāvantettha samāgatā; Taṇhāya mūlaṁ khaṇatha, usīratthova bīraṇaṁ; Mā vo naḷaṁva sotova, māro bhañji punappunaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: beginner · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md), [Māra](../obstacles/mara.md)

### 338 <a id="tea-dhammapada-338"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

As a tree, even when cut, grows again if its root is firm and unharmed, so, when the latent tendency of craving (taṇhānusaya) is not uprooted, this suffering (dukkha) arises again and again.

> Sūkarapotikāvatthu Yathāpi mūle anupaddave daḷhe, Chinnopi rukkho punareva rūhati; Evampi taṇhānusaye anūhate, Nibbattatī dukkhamidaṁ punappunaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, consciousness-mind_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [anusaya](../terms/anusaya.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md)

### 339 <a id="tea-dhammapada-339"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

One whose thirty-six streams (sotā) flow strongly towards what is pleasing (manāpasavanā): the thoughts based on passion (rāganissita saṅkappā) carry away that person of wrong view (duddiṭṭhi).

> Yassa chattiṁsati sotā, manāpasavanā bhusā; Mahāvahanti duddiṭṭhiṁ, saṅkappā rāganissitā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [sota](../terms/sota-stream.md), [saṅkappa](../terms/sankappa.md), [rāga](../terms/raga.md) · obstacles: [Passion (rāga)](../obstacles/raga-pali.md)

### 340 <a id="tea-dhammapada-340"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

The streams flow everywhere; the creeper (latā), once sprung up, stands; seeing that creeper arisen, cut its root with wisdom (paññā).

> Savanti sabbadhi sotā, latā uppajja tiṭṭhati; Tañca disvā lataṁ jātaṁ, mūlaṁ paññāya chindatha.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: knowledge · stage: intermediate · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

terms: [sota](../terms/sota-stream.md), [latā](../terms/lata.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md)

### 341 <a id="tea-dhammapada-341"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Flowing (saritāni) and moistened (sinehitāni) are the pleasures (somanassāni) of a person; those who are attached to ease and seek pleasure (sukhesino) — such people are subject to birth and ageing.

> Saritāni sinehitāni ca, Somanassāni bhavanti jantuno; Te sātasitā sukhesino, Te ve jātijarūpagā narā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [somanassa](../terms/somanassa.md), [sukha](../terms/sukha.md), [jāti](../terms/jati.md), [jarā](../terms/jara.md)

### 342 <a id="tea-dhammapada-342"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

People driven by craving (tasiṇā) scurry about like a trapped hare; bound by fetters and attachments, they come to suffering again and again for a long time.

> Tasiṇāya purakkhatā pajā, Parisappanti sasova bandhito; Saṁyojanasaṅgasattakā, Dukkhamupenti punappunaṁ cirāya.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, karma-liberation_

terms: [tasiṇā](../terms/tasina.md), [saṁyojana](../terms/samyojana.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md)

### 343 <a id="tea-dhammapada-343"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

People driven by craving (tasiṇā) scurry about like a trapped hare; therefore one who wishes for his own dispassion (virāga) should dispel craving.

> Tasiṇāya purakkhatā pajā, Parisappanti sasova bandhito; Tasmā tasiṇaṁ vinodaye, Ākaṅkhanta virāgamattano.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [tasiṇā](../terms/tasina.md), [virāga](../terms/viraga.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md)

### 344 <a id="tea-dhammapada-344"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

One who, free from the undergrowth (vana-tha), is nevertheless inclined to the forest (vana) — freed from the forest, runs to the very forest: look at that person; freed, he runs to bondage itself.

> Vibbhantabhikkhuvatthu Yo nibbanatho vanādhimutto, Vanamutto vanameva dhāvati; Taṁ puggalametha passatha, Mutto bandhanameva dhāvati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [vana](../terms/vana.md), [vanatha](../terms/vanatha.md), [bandhana](../terms/bandhana.md)

### 345 <a id="tea-dhammapada-345"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

The steadfast do not call that a strong bond which is of iron, wood or grass rope; but the intense passion (sārattarattā) for jewels and earrings, and the longing (apekkhā) for sons and wives, (they do call strong).

> Bandhanāgāravatthu Na taṁ daḷhaṁ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, Yadāyasaṁ dārujapabbajañca; Sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalesu, Puttesu dāresu ca yā apekkhā.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [bandhana](../terms/bandhana.md), [apekkhā](../terms/apekkha.md)

### 346 <a id="tea-dhammapada-346"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

This the steadfast call a strong bond: dragging down, slack, hard to release; having cut even this, they go forth, without longing (anapekkhī), leaving sensual pleasure.

> Etaṁ daḷhaṁ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, Ohārinaṁ sithilaṁ duppamuñcaṁ; Etampi chetvāna paribbajanti, Anapekkhino kāmasukhaṁ pahāya.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind, practice_

terms: [bandhana](../terms/bandhana.md), [apekkhā](../terms/apekkha.md), [kāma](../terms/kama.md)

### 347 <a id="tea-dhammapada-347"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Those inflamed with passion (rāga) fall into the stream, like a spider into the web it has made itself; having cut even this, the steadfast go on, without longing, abandoning all suffering.

> Khemātherīvatthu Ye rāgarattānupatanti sotaṁ, Sayaṅkataṁ makkaṭakova jālaṁ; Etampi chetvāna vajanti dhīrā, Anapekkhino sabbadukkhaṁ pahāya.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [rāga](../terms/raga.md), [sota](../terms/sota-stream.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md) · obstacles: [Passion (rāga)](../obstacles/raga-pali.md)

### 348 <a id="tea-dhammapada-348"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Let go of the before, let go of the after, let go of the middle, going beyond existence (bhava); with a mind freed everywhere, you will not again come to birth and ageing.

> Uggasenavatthu Muñca pure muñca pacchato, Majjhe muñca bhavassa pāragū; Sabbattha vimuttamānaso, Na punaṁ jātijaraṁ upehisi.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: advanced · types: practice, karma-liberation_

terms: [bhāva](../terms/bhava.md), [vimutta](../terms/vimutta.md), [jāti](../terms/jati.md), [jarā](../terms/jara.md)

### 349 <a id="tea-dhammapada-349"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

For a person tormented by thoughts (vitakka), of strong passion (tibbarāga), who dwells on the beautiful (subhānupassī): craving increases still more; he indeed makes his bond strong.

> Cūḷadhanuggahapaṇḍitavatthu Vitakkamathitassa jantuno, Tibbarāgassa subhānupassino; Bhiyyo taṇhā pavaḍḍhati, Esa kho daḷhaṁ karoti bandhanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: consciousness-mind_

terms: [vitakka](../terms/vitakka.md), [rāga](../terms/raga.md), [subha](../terms/subha.md), [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [bandhana](../terms/bandhana.md) · obstacles: [Passion (rāga)](../obstacles/raga-pali.md), [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md)

### 350 <a id="tea-dhammapada-350"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

But whoever delights in the calming of thoughts (vitakkūpasama) and is always mindful (sata) in cultivating the unlovely (asubha): he will make an end (of craving), he will cut Māra's bond (mārabandhana).

> Vitakkūpasame ca yo rato, Asubhaṁ bhāvayate sadā sato; Esa kho byanti kāhiti, Esa checchati mārabandhanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [vitakka](../terms/vitakka.md), [asubha](../terms/asubha.md), [sati](../terms/sati.md), [māra](../terms/mara-pali.md), [bandhana](../terms/bandhana.md) · practices: [Contemplation of the foul (asubha-bhāvanā): summary only](../practices/asubha-bhavana.md)

### 351 <a id="tea-dhammapada-351"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who has reached the goal (niṭṭhaṅgata), fearless (asantāsī), free of craving (vītataṇha), blameless, has cut off the darts of existence (bhavasallā); this body is his last.

> Māravatthu Niṭṭhaṅgato asantāsī, vītataṇho anaṅgaṇo; Acchindi bhavasallāni, antimoyaṁ samussayo.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: general · stage: realized · types: karma-liberation_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [bhāva](../terms/bhava.md)

### 352 <a id="tea-dhammapada-352"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

Free of craving, without grasping (anādāna), skilled in words and expression (niruttipada), knowing the combination of the syllables and what comes before and after: he is called one with his last body (antimasārīra), of great wisdom, a great person.

> Vītataṇho anādāno, Niruttipadakovido; Akkharānaṁ sannipātaṁ, Jaññā pubbāparāni ca; Sa ve “antimasārīro, Mahāpañño mahāpuriso”ti vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: realized · types: karma-liberation, sound-language_

terms: [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md), [niruttipada](../terms/niruttipada.md), [paññā](../terms/panna.md)

### 353 <a id="tea-dhammapada-353"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

'I am the conqueror of all, the knower of all; among all dhammas I am unsmeared; I have abandoned all, and am freed in the destruction of craving (taṇhakkhaya); having understood for myself, whom should I point to (as teacher)?'

> Upakājīvakavatthu Sabbābhibhū sabbavidūhamasmi, Sabbesu dhammesu anūpalitto; Sabbañjaho taṇhakkhaye vimutto, Sayaṁ abhiññāya kamuddiseyyaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: absolute · path: general · stage: realized · types: ultimate, karma-liberation_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [taṇhakkhaya](../terms/tanhakkhaya.md)

### 354 <a id="tea-dhammapada-354"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The gift of the Dhamma (dhammadāna) surpasses all gifts; the taste of the Dhamma (dhammaraso) surpasses all tastes; delight in the Dhamma (dhammarati) surpasses all delights; the destruction of craving (taṇhakkhaya) overcomes all suffering.

> Sakkapañhavatthu Sabbadānaṁ dhammadānaṁ jināti, Sabbarasaṁ dhammaraso jināti; Sabbaratiṁ dhammarati jināti, Taṇhakkhayo sabbadukkhaṁ jināti.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: all · types: karma-liberation, ultimate_

terms: [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [dāna](../terms/dana.md), [taṇhakkhaya](../terms/tanhakkhaya.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 355 <a id="tea-dhammapada-355"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Riches destroy the dull-witted, but not those who seek the far shore (pāra); through craving for riches the dull-witted destroys himself as if he were another.

> Aputtakaseṭṭhivatthu Hananti bhogā dummedhaṁ, no ca pāragavesino; Bhogataṇhāya dummedho, hanti aññeva attanaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

terms: [bhoga](../terms/bhoga-pali.md), [taṇhā](../terms/tanha.md) · obstacles: [Craving (taṇhā)](../obstacles/tanha.md)

### 356 <a id="tea-dhammapada-356"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Fields have weeds as their fault; this people has passion (rāga) as its fault; therefore what is given to those free of passion (vītarāga) is of great fruit.

> Aṅkuravatthu Tiṇadosāni khettāni, rāgadosā ayaṁ pajā; Tasmā hi vītarāgesu, dinnaṁ hoti mahapphalaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: causal · path: general · stage: all · types: ethics, karma-liberation_

### 357 <a id="tea-dhammapada-357"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Fields have weeds as their fault; this people has hatred (dosa) as its fault; therefore what is given to those free of hatred is of great fruit.

> Tiṇadosāni khettāni, dosadosā ayaṁ pajā; Tasmā hi vītadosesu, dinnaṁ hoti mahapphalaṁ.

### 358 <a id="tea-dhammapada-358"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Fields have weeds as their fault; this people has delusion (moha) as its fault; therefore what is given to those free of delusion is of great fruit.

> Tiṇadosāni khettāni, mohadosā ayaṁ pajā; Tasmā hi vītamohesu, dinnaṁ hoti mahapphalaṁ.

terms: [moha](../terms/moha.md), [dāna](../terms/dana.md) · practices: [Giving (dāna)](../practices/dana.md) · obstacles: [Delusion (moha)](../obstacles/moha.md)

### 359 <a id="tea-dhammapada-359"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Fields have weeds as their fault; this people has desire (icchā) as its fault; therefore what is given to those free of desire is of great fruit.

> Tiṇadosāni khettāni, icchādosā ayaṁ pajā; Tasmā hi vigaticchesu, dinnaṁ hoti mahapphalaṁ. Taṇhāvaggo catuvīsatimo.

### 360 <a id="tea-dhammapada-360"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Restraint with the eye is good; good is restraint with the ear; good is restraint with the nose; good is restraint with the tongue.

> Cakkhunā saṁvaro sādhu, sādhu sotena saṁvaro; Ghānena saṁvaro sādhu, sādhu jivhāya saṁvaro.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [saṃvara](../terms/samvara-pali.md) · practices: [Sense restraint (indriyasaṃvara)](../practices/indriya-samvara.md)

### 361 <a id="tea-dhammapada-361"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Restraint with the body is good, good is restraint with speech, good is restraint with the mind; restraint everywhere is good; a bhikkhu restrained everywhere is freed from all suffering.

> Kāyena saṁvaro sādhu, sādhu vācāya saṁvaro; Manasā saṁvaro sādhu, sādhu sabbattha saṁvaro; Sabbattha saṁvuto bhikkhu, sabbadukkhā pamuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [saṃvara](../terms/samvara-pali.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [dukkha](../terms/dukkha.md)

### 362 <a id="tea-dhammapada-362"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Restrained in hand, restrained in foot, restrained in speech, the best of the restrained, delighting inwardly (ajjhattarata), composed, alone, content: him they call a bhikkhu.

> Haṁsaghātakabhikkhuvatthu Hatthasaṁyato pādasaṁyato, Vācāsaṁyato saṁyatuttamo; Ajjhattarato samāhito, Eko santusito tamāhu bhikkhuṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, ethics_

terms: [ajjhattarata](../terms/ajjhattarata.md), [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [santuṭṭhi](../terms/santutthi.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md)

### 363 <a id="tea-dhammapada-363"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The bhikkhu who is restrained in mouth, speaks with insight (mantabhāṇī), is not restless (anuddhata), and illuminates the meaning (attha) and the Dhamma: sweet is his speech.

> Kokālikavatthu Yo mukhasaṁyato bhikkhu, mantabhāṇī anuddhato; Atthaṁ dhammañca dīpeti, madhuraṁ tassa bhāsitaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: ethics, sound-language_

terms: [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [attha](../terms/attha.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md)

### 364 <a id="tea-dhammapada-364"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The bhikkhu who has the Dhamma as his garden (dhammārāma), delights in the Dhamma, reflects on the Dhamma and recollects the Dhamma (dhammaṁ anussaraṁ), does not fall away from the true Dhamma.

> Dhammārāmattheravatthu Dhammārāmo dhammarato, dhammaṁ anuvicintayaṁ; Dhammaṁ anussaraṁ bhikkhu, saddhammā na parihāyati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

terms: [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [dhamma](../terms/dhamma.md), [saddhamma](../terms/saddhamma.md) · practices: [Recollection of the Dhamma](../practices/dhammanussati.md)

### 365 <a id="tea-dhammapada-365"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One should not despise one's own gains, nor go about envying others; a bhikkhu who envies others does not attain concentration (samādhi).

> Vipakkhasevakabhikkhuvatthu Salābhaṁ nātimaññeyya, nāññesaṁ pihayaṁ care; Aññesaṁ pihayaṁ bhikkhu, samādhiṁ nādhigacchati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: ethics, consciousness-mind_

terms: [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [samādhi](../terms/samadhi.md) · obstacles: [Envy (issā)](../obstacles/issa.md)

### 366 <a id="tea-dhammapada-366"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Even if a bhikkhu receives little, if he does not despise his gains, the gods praise him, of pure livelihood and unwearied.

> Appalābhopi ce bhikkhu, salābhaṁ nātimaññati; Taṁ ve devā pasaṁsanti, suddhājīviṁ atanditaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: ethics_

terms: [bhikkhu](../terms/bhikkhu.md), [deva](../terms/deva-pali.md)

### 367 <a id="tea-dhammapada-367"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

One who has no sense of 'mine' (mamāyita) at all in name-and-form (nāmarūpa), and who does not grieve for what is not (asat), he is indeed called a bhikkhu.

> Pañcaggadāyakabrāhmaṇavatthu Sabbaso nāmarūpasmiṁ, yassa natthi mamāyitaṁ; Asatā ca na socati, sa ve “bhikkhū”ti vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: realized · types: consciousness-mind_

### 368 <a id="tea-dhammapada-368"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

The bhikkhu who abides in loving-kindness (mettāvihārī) and is confident in the Buddha's teaching (buddhasāsana) would reach the peaceful state (santaṁ padaṁ), the stilling of formations (saṅkhārūpasama), happiness.

> Sambahulabhikkhuvatthu Mettāvihārī yo bhikkhu, pasanno buddhasāsane; Adhigacche padaṁ santaṁ, saṅkhārūpasamaṁ sukhaṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

### 369 <a id="tea-dhammapada-369"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Bail out this boat, bhikkhu; bailed, it will go lightly for you; having cut passion (rāga) and hatred (dosa), you will then go to nibbāna.

> Siñca bhikkhu imaṁ nāvaṁ, sittā te lahumessati; Chetvā rāgañca dosañca, tato nibbānamehisi.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

### 370 <a id="tea-dhammapada-370"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence low

Cut five, abandon five, and develop five further; a bhikkhu who has gone beyond the five attachments (pañca saṅga) is called one who has crossed the flood (oghatiṇṇa).

> Pañca chinde pañca jahe, pañca cuttari bhāvaye; Pañca saṅgātigo bhikkhu, “oghatiṇṇo”ti vuccati.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: advanced · types: practice, karma-liberation_

### 371 <a id="tea-dhammapada-371"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Meditate (jhāya), bhikkhu, do not be heedless; let not your mind delight in the strands of sensual pleasure (kāmaguṇa); do not, heedless, swallow an iron ball; do not cry out 'this is suffering!' as you burn.

> Jhāya bhikkhu mā pamādo, Mā te kāmaguṇe ramessu cittaṁ; Mā lohaguḷaṁ gilī pamatto, Mā kandi “dukkhamidan”ti dayhamāno.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice_

### 372 <a id="tea-dhammapada-372"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

There is no jhāna for one without wisdom, no wisdom for one without jhāna; one in whom are both jhāna and wisdom is close to nibbāna.

> Natthi jhānaṁ apaññassa, paññā natthi ajhāyato; Yamhi jhānañca paññā ca, sa ve nibbānasantike.

_level: conventional · standpoint: analytic · path: meditation, knowledge · stage: advanced · types: practice, consciousness-mind_

### 373 <a id="tea-dhammapada-373"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence moderate

For the bhikkhu who has entered an empty dwelling (suññāgāra), whose mind is peaceful, who sees the Dhamma rightly (sammā dhammaṁ vipassato), there is a delight beyond the human (amānusī rati).

> Suññāgāraṁ paviṭṭhassa, santacittassa bhikkhuno; Amānusī rati hoti, sammā dhammaṁ vipassato.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, powers-experiences_

### 374 <a id="tea-dhammapada-374"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Whenever he contemplates (sammasati) the rise and fall (udayabbaya) of the aggregates (khandha), he gains joy and gladness (pītipāmojja); that is the deathless (amata) for those who know.

> Yato yato sammasati, khandhānaṁ udayabbayaṁ; Labhatī pītipāmojjaṁ, amataṁ taṁ vijānataṁ.

_level: conventional · standpoint: experiential · path: meditation · stage: intermediate · types: practice, powers-experiences_

### 375 <a id="tea-dhammapada-375"></a>
`text-verified` · confidence high

Here is the beginning for the wise bhikkhu: guarding the sense faculties (indriyagutti), contentment (santuṭṭhi), and restraint in the Pātimokkha.

> Tatrāyamādi bhavati, idha paññassa bhikkhuno; Indriyagutti santuṭṭhi, pātimokkhe ca saṁvaro.

_level: conventional · standpoint: seeker · path: general · stage: beginner · types: practice_
